Result: 1. 基于状态的下一个策略证明器（state-based next-tactic prover）天然满足证明等变性。2. 当前最先进的LLM-based证明器既不满足证明等变性也不满足成功不变性，在等价表述上表现出巨大的性能差异。3. 提出的测试时聚合方法在固定推理预算下能提升鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 对称性是LLM-based定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置（inductive bias），测试时计算是近似实现对称性的实用途径。

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>


### [107] [An Evidence Hierarchy for Bayesian Object Classification via OSINT-Aided Heterogeneous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22259)
*Jan Nausner,Michael Hubner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种上下文感知和领域知识增强的异构传感器融合方法，通过构建证据层次结构、引入OSINT上下文信息、设计贝叶斯威胁分类机制，在模拟场景中实现了高达95%的分类准确率，有效应对CBRNE威胁检测中的传感器局限性和杂波问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构传感器在CBRNE威胁检测中存在感知能力有限、可靠性不一、杂波率高以及高质量数据集匮乏等问题，亟需一种能够融合多源信息、提升威胁分类鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 1) 建立新型证据层次结构，对直接、指示性和上下文信息进行分层建模；2) 引入OSINT数据收集与处理，将环境上下文信息融入融合过程；3) 基于领域知识构建先验，设计贝叶斯威胁类型分类机制。

Result: 在模拟场景下的评估表明，该方法在杂波和先验失配情况下具有鲁棒性，整体分类准确率最高达95%。

Conclusion: 所提出的上下文感知与领域知识增强的融合方法能有效克服传感器相关缺陷，提升CBRNE威胁检测与分类性能。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sensor fusion is vital for detecting, localizing, and classifying CBRNE threats. However, individual sensors are often only capable of detecting a subset of relevant threats with varying reliability or can even provide only indirect threat indications, making threat classification challenging. Furthermore, high clutter rates on the sensor side present a great challenge for fusion systems. Additionally, the limited availability of high quality datasets hinders the advancement of learning-based detection and classification models in smart sensors. To mitigate these sensor related shortcomings, a context-aware and domain knowledge-enhanced fusion process is proposed. First, a novel evidence hierarchy is established that enables modeling of direct, indicative, and contextual information. Second, contextual information about the environment is introduced into the fusion process, by collecting, processing, and exploiting OSINT inputs. Third, all levels of the evidence hierarchy are used to craft a Bayesian threat type classification mechanism with domain knowledge-informed priors. The proposed methodology is evaluated in simulated scenarios, and the results demonstrate the benefit of the proposed fusion approach in terms of robustness to clutter and prior mismatch, with an overall classification accuracy of up to 95%.

</details>


### [108] [Tailoring Teaching to Aptitude: Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22263)
*Hongbin Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Kehai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Jinpeng Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），通过熵路由的定向监督解决同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）抑制探索和推理退化的问题，在数学推理任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD通过特权信息提供密集token级监督，但会抑制预测不确定性（高熵token的探索）并降低步骤精度（低熵token的准确性），导致复杂推理退化。

Method: 提出DASD，将均匀的教师模仿重构为熵路由的定向监督：高熵token推离特权教师以保持探索，低熵token拉向教师以稳定步骤执行。

Result: 在6个数学推理基准上，DASD的宏Avg@16优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线。Pass@k、推理健康度和泛化分析表明性能提升源于保留探索能力且不牺牲步骤执行。

Conclusion: DASD通过熵自适应的方向性监督解决了OPSD在探索与执行之间的权衡，为LLM后训练提供了更有效的自蒸馏范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is an emerging LLM post-training paradigm in which the model serves as its own teacher: conditioned on privileged information such as a reference trace or hint, the same policy provides dense token-level supervision on its own rollouts. However, recent studies show that OPSD degrades complex reasoning by suppressing predictive uncertainty, which supports exploration and hypothesis revision. Our token-level analysis shows that this failure arises from applying a uniform direction of teacher supervision across tokens with different uncertainty levels: conformity to the privileged self-teacher suppresses exploration at high entropy, while deviation from the teacher degrades step accuracy at low entropy. Accordingly, we propose \textbf{Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation} (\textbf{DASD}), which reframes privileged self-distillation from uniform teacher imitation into entropy-routed directional supervision: high-entropy tokens are pushed away from the privileged teacher to preserve exploration, while low-entropy tokens are pulled toward the teacher to stabilize step-level execution. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DASD achieves the best macro Avg@16 over strong RLVR and self-distillation baselines. Pass@$k$, reasoning-health, and generalization analyses show that these average gains come from preserving exploration without sacrificing step-level execution.

</details>


### [109] [Detecting Atypical Clients in Federated Learning via Representation-Level Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22266)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quitana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级几何信号，通过衡量客户端本地训练如何改变输入空间的激活诱导划分（基于共享探针集），来量化客户端与全局模型的功能偏差。该指标能有效区分稳定的异构客户端与产生异常功能变化的客户端，为联邦学习提供风险感知的聚合监控工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构性导致更新不稳定、全局性能下降；同时实际部署中客户端更新可能因分布偏移或异常输入偏离预期行为，现有方法难以有效区分良性异构与异常偏离，亟需一种可靠的监控信号。

Method: 提出一种不依赖模型参数或梯度的轻量级几何信号：通过共享探针集衡量每个客户端本地训练如何改变输入空间的激活诱导分区，从而得到置换不变、可解释的客户端-全局差异度量。

Result: 该信号能有效识别引发异常功能变化的客户端，区分稳定的异构客户端与显著偏离全局模式的更新。

Conclusion: 该指标为联邦学习系统提供了一种简单的客户端行为监控工具，支持风险感知的聚合策略。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training across distributed clients with heterogeneous data, but such heterogeneity often leads to unstable updates and degraded global performance. Moreover, in practical deployments, client updates may deviate from the expected behavior not only due to benign not i.i.d. distributions, but also due to distributional shifts or anomalous inputs, raising concerns about the reliability of the aggregation process. In this work, we propose a lightweight geometric signal to quantify the functional deviation of a client with respect to the global model. Instead of comparing model parameters or gradients, our approach measures how the local training of each client alters the activation-induced partition of the input space, evaluated on a shared probe set. This yields a permutation-invariant, interpretable metric of client--global divergence that captures differences in how data is processed by the model. We show that this signal effectively identifies clients that induce atypical functional changes, distinguishing stable yet heterogeneous clients from those whose updates significantly diverge from the global regime. As a result, the proposed metric provides a simple tool for monitoring client behavior and enabling risk-aware aggregation strategies in federated learning systems.

</details>


### [110] [Adaptive Measurement Allocation for Learning Kernelized SVMs Under Noisy Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22275)
*Artur Miroszewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应测量分配策略，用于从带噪伯努利观测中学习核化支持向量机，该方法结合几何敏感性和活动集不稳定性两种互补原则，将有限测量预算集中分配在最关键的核矩阵区域，理论分析和实验表明其显著优于均匀分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法假设核矩阵可无噪声精确获得，但在量子机器学习等新兴场景中，每个核条目需从带噪观测中推断，且精度取决于有限测量预算的分配方式。现有方法普遍采用均匀分配，忽略了核化分类器对核矩阵高度非均匀的依赖性。

Method: 提出自适应测量分配策略：(i) 几何敏感性——捕捉单个核条目扰动对分类器间隔的影响；(ii) 活动集不稳定性——量化测量噪声导致支持向量归属离散变化的概率。二者结合定义任务感知的分配方案，将测量集中到核矩阵中最决策关键的区域。

Result: 理论分析表明自适应分配的优势由核重要性结构的异质性决定，存在自适应或均匀策略各自更优的不同区间。合成数据实验证明自适应分配在固定测量预算下显著提升支持向量恢复、间隔估计和决策函数精度。对偶系数稳定性准则实现早停，用部分测量成本达到近最优性能。真实数据量子核实验呈现与核集中等已知现象一致的区间依赖性行为。

Conclusion: 自适应测量分配策略能有效提升带噪核SVM学习性能，其优势取决于核重要性结构的异质性程度，并可在量子机器学习等核矩阵需从噪声观测推断的场景中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Kernel methods are typically formulated under the assumption of exact, noise-free access to the Gram matrix. However, in emerging settings such as quantum machine learning, each kernel entry must be inferred from noisy observations, and its accuracy depends on how a limited measurement budget is allocated. Despite this, existing approaches overwhelmingly rely on uniform allocation, which equalizes estimator variance but ignores the highly non-uniform dependence of kernelized classifiers on the Gram matrix. In this work, we introduce an adaptive measurement-allocation strategy for learning kernelized Support Vector Machines (SVMs) from noisy Bernoulli observations. Our approach combines two complementary principles: (i) geometric sensitivity, capturing how perturbations of individual kernel entries affect the classifier margin, and (ii) active-set instability, quantifying the probability of discrete changes in support-vector membership induced by measurement noise. These signals define a task-aware allocation scheme that concentrates measurements on the most decision-critical regions of the kernel matrix. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that the benefit of adaptive allocation is governed by the heterogeneity of the induced kernel importance structure, leading to distinct regimes in which adaptive or uniform strategies are preferable. Empirical evaluations on synthetic datasets demonstrate that adaptive allocation significantly improves support-vector recovery, margin estimation, and decision-function accuracy under fixed measurement budgets. A dual-coefficient stability criterion further enables early stopping, achieving near-optimal performance while using only a fraction of the measurement cost. Additional experiments on quantum kernels derived from real-world data reveal a regime-dependent behavior aligned with known phenomena such as kernel concentration. Together...

</details>


### [111] [EmoTrack: Robust Depression Tracking from Counseling Transcripts across Session Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22286)
*Zhaomin Wu,Jiayi Li,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel多会话数据集和EmoTrack框架，用于从心理咨询文本中鲁棒预测PHQ-8抑郁评分，结合LLM提取的临床信号与冻结的语义嵌入，在单会话和多会话场景下均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的AI心理健康支持需要准确的抑郁严重度监测，但现有方法难以同时兼顾单会话和多会话场景的鲁棒性：微调方法在数据稀缺时泛化差，提示方法缺乏纵向上下文支持。

Method: 提出EmoTrack框架，将LLM提取的临床信号与冻结的逐轮语义嵌入相结合，训练症状特异性预测器；对于多会话场景，引入紧凑的跨会话记忆机制。同时构建LongCounsel数据集，包含会话级PHQ-8标注，支持跨会话连续性和部分症状披露评估。

Result: 在LongCounsel和DAIC-WOZ基准上，EmoTrack在单会话场景中实现13.5%的相对MAE降低，在多会话场景中与最强基线持平。

Conclusion: 结合LLM临床信号提取与冻结语义嵌入的方法能有效提升抑郁追踪的鲁棒性，跨会话记忆机制有助于利用纵向上下文信息。

Abstract: Text-based counseling is an important interface for AI mental-health support, where transcripts may be used to monitor depression severity and flag sessions requiring timely human review. However, robust PHQ-8 prediction across session regimes remains challenging: fine-tuning-based methods can exploit richer supervision but may generalize poorly under data scarcity, while prompt-based LLM methods are data-efficient but usually treat each transcript holistically and provide limited support for longitudinal context. We study robust depression tracking from counseling transcripts across single-session and multi-session regimes. We introduce LongCounsel, a multi-session counseling dataset with session-level PHQ-8 supervision for evaluating repeated-session tracking under partial symptom disclosure and cross-session continuity. We further propose EmoTrack, a PHQ-8 prediction framework that combines LLM-extracted clinical signals with frozen turn-level semantic embeddings and trains symptom-specific predictors over the resulting transcript representation. When prior sessions are available, EmoTrack can further incorporate them through compact cross-session memory. Experiments on LongCounsel and DAIC-WOZ show that EmoTrack achieves a clear gain on the real single-session benchmark, including a 13.5% relative MAE reduction over the strongest DAIC-WOZ baseline, and remains competitive with the strongest longitudinal baseline on LongCounsel.

</details>


### [112] [Long-term Fairness with Selective Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22291)
*Giovani Valdrighi,Isabel Valera,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究选择性标签（如仅对获批贷款者已知的还款能力）下的长期公平性问题，提出通过分解观测公平性与标签预测偏差来估计真实公平性，并基于此设计强化学习算法，在半合成环境中达到接近理想标签Oracle的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期公平性算法假设标签完全可观测，但在招聘、贷款等场景中标签是选择性的（仅对获得积极决策的个体可见），直接应用朴素方法无法保证公平性，亟需针对这一实际约束的理论框架与算法。

Method: 将真实公平性指标分解为可观测公平性部分与标签预测偏差部分，利用标签预测模型的置信度导出充分条件，使公平性可从可观测量中保证；在此基础上提出结合观测数据与预测模型的强化学习算法。

Result: 在半合成环境中，所提算法在公平性和性能指标上与拥有真实标签Oracle的智能体达到可比水平。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能处理选择性标签下长期公平决策的理论框架与可行算法，有效缓解了标签缺失带来的公平性偏差。

Abstract: Long-term fairness algorithms aim to satisfy fairness beyond static and short-term notions by accounting for the dynamics between decision-making policies and population behavior. Most previous approaches evaluate performance and fairness measures from observable features and a label, which is assumed to be fully observed. However, in scenarios such as hiring or lending, the labels (e.g., ability to repay the loan) are selective labels as they are only revealed based on positive decisions (e.g., when a loan is granted). In this paper, we study long-term fairness in the selective labels setting and analytically show that naive solutions do not guarantee fairness. To address this gap, we then introduce a novel framework that leverages both the observed data and a label predictor model to estimate the true fairness measure value by decomposing it into the observed fairness and bias from label predictions. This allows us to derive sufficient conditions to satisfy true fairness from observable quantities by using the confidence in the predictor model. Finally, we rely on our theoretical results to propose a novel reinforcement learning algorithm for effective long-term fair decision-making with selective labels. In semisynthetic environments, the proposed algorithm reached comparable fairness and performance to an agent with oracle access to the true labels.

</details>


### [113] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR)，基于重尾自正则化（HT-SR）理论为Transformer各层分配不同的学习率：弱重尾层用大学习率加速训练，强重尾层用小学习率。实验表明LLR在多种架构和优化器下实现1.5倍训练加速，零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，且调优开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习中对所有层使用统一学习率的做法忽略了Transformer各层的结构异质性，可能限制其在大语言模型中的有效性。

Method: 基于HT-SR理论，通过权重相关矩阵的经验谱密度（ESD）量化各层的重尾程度，为弱重尾层分配大学习率加速训练，强重尾层分配小学习率，实现层间均衡训练。

Result: 在LLaMA、GPT-nano等多种架构、AdamW和Muon优化器、60M-1B参数规模下，LLR实现最高1.5倍训练加速，平均零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，且超参数可从统一基线直接迁移。

Conclusion: 基于层间异质性自适应分配学习率能够有效加速Transformer训练并提升泛化性能，且调优成本极低。

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate their training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures (from LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and parameter scales (60M-1B) demonstrate that LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and outperforms baselines, notably raising average zero-shot accuracy from 47.09% to 49.02%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it transfers nearly optimal LR settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [114] [Chebyshev Policies and the Mountain Car Problem: Reinforcement Learning for Low-Dimensional Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22305)
*Stefan Huber,Hannes Unger,Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过解析求解Mountain Car问题的36年未解最优控制解，揭示了现代RL算法与最优解之间存在巨大差距，并基于此提出了Chebyshev策略——一种可替代神经网络的通用策略表示方法，在多个任务上以更少参数实现了显著更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭合经典RL基准问题Mountain Car的最优控制解这一长期未解缺口，并借此揭示现代RL智能体与最优策略之间的性能差距，从而推动更高效、可解释的策略表示方法研究。

Method: 1) 解析求解Mountain Car问题的最优控制；2) 基于最优控制分析，从第一性原理出发引入Chebyshev多项式作为策略表示（Chebyshev policies）；3) 作为神经网络的即插即用替代方案，与PPO、ARS、REINFORCE等算法结合进行训练。

Result: Chebyshev策略在Mountain Car上将遗憾降低了4.18倍，同时参数数量减少277倍；在多个RL任务（包括真实世界非线性运动控制测试平台）上，与PPO、ARS、REINFORCE结合时均一致优于神经网络。

Conclusion: Chebyshev策略是低维控制任务中神经网络的一种有说服力且轻量级的替代方案或补充，能够提升样本效率、可解释性和实时性。

Abstract: We analytically solve the Mountain Car problem, a canonical benchmark in RL, and derive an optimal control solution, closing a gap after 36 years. This enables us to reveal two surprising insights: The optimal control is quite simple, yet modern RL agents display a large gap to optimality. Motivated by the analysis of the optimal control, we introduce Chebyshev policies as a universal (i.e. dense) class of RL policies from first principles. They can be trained as drop-in replacements of neural nets, reducing the regret by a factor of 4.18, while requiring 277 times fewer parameters, fostering sample efficiency, explainability and realtime capability. Chebyshev policies are evaluated on further RL tasks, including a real-world nonlinear motion control testbed. They consistently improve performance over neural nets with PPO, ARS and REINFORCE. Our results demonstrate how Chebyshev policies offer a compelling and lightweight alternative or addition to neural nets for low-dimensional control tasks.

</details>


### [115] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SepsisAI-Orchestrator，一个开源模块化平台，解决早期脓毒症检测模型从研究到临床部署的工程障碍。平台整合HL7 FHIR风格的CDA预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM分类器（REST API）、Streamlit临床仪表盘，并通过Docker和Kubernetes编排。在12线程CPU上的负载测试发现，副本数需与物理CPU线程数匹配：从3副本增至12副本，p95延迟降低57.3%（3.3s→1.41s）并消除请求失败；过度扩展至24/48副本反而因调度竞争导致性能下降，首次量化了临床AI推理负载的U型扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习模型虽有强预测性能，但因异质数据表示、缺乏标准化部署流程、以及研究原型与医院环境并发/延迟需求不匹配等系统性障碍，模型向床旁转化受限。本文旨在弥合这一部署鸿沟。

Method: 构建开源模块化平台SepsisAI-Orchestrator，整合HL7 FHIR风格的CDA预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM分类器（REST API）、Streamlit仪表盘，基于Docker和Kubernetes编排。使用k6工具进行50-1000并发虚拟用户的负载测试，系统性地改变副本数（3/6/12/24/48）并测量p95延迟和请求失败率。

Result: 副本数需匹配宿主机的物理CPU线程数（12线程）。从3副本增加到12副本，p95延迟从3.3s降至1.41s（降57.3%），所有请求失败消除。但过配置至24或48副本时，因调度器竞争导致性能退化，表现出U型扩展行为，这在临床AI推理工作负载中此前未被量化。

Conclusion: SepsisAI-Orchestrator展示了将已有临床模型可靠部署至生产环境的工程方案；负载测试揭示了容器副本数需与物理CPU线程数精确匹配的U型扩展规律，为临床AI系统资源配置提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [116] [Riemannian geometry meets fMRI: the advantages of modeling correlation manifolds and eigenvector subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22334)
*Mario Severino,Manuela Moretto,Robert A. McCutcheon,Mattia Veronese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的几何框架用于功能脑网络相关性矩阵分析，包括Off-log度量（将相关矩阵映射为对称零对角矩阵，支持闭式距离、Frechet均值和线性模型）和Grassmannian子空间判别（通过主角度距离比较特征向量子空间，解决符号和基模糊性）。在两个临床队列（帕金森病、精神病）和三个老化fMRI数据集上验证，Off-log度量提高了置换检验敏感性，Grassmannian方法持续优于欧几里得基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准相关性矩阵分析将条目视为独立，忽略了相关空间的弯曲几何结构；现有几何方法缺乏闭式运算或依赖任意区域排序，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出双组件几何框架：(i) Off-log度量——一种光滑变换，将相关矩阵映射为对称零对角矩阵，实现距离、Frechet均值和线性模型的闭式表达式；(ii) Grassmannian子空间判别——通过特征向量子空间间的主角度距离比较被试，解决符号和基模糊性。

Result: 在帕金森病和精神病两个临床队列及三个老化fMRI数据集上，Off-log度量提高了置换检验敏感性，分类性能匹配或超越黎曼和欧几里得基线；脑龄预测性能与基线相当；Grassmannian方法持续优于欧几里得基线，突出疾病相关网络。

Conclusion: 几何感知表示能提高敏感性和预测性能，同时保持可大规模部署的简便性。

Abstract: Correlation matrices are fundamental summaries of functional brain networks, yet standard analyses often treat entries independently, ignoring the curved geometry of correlation space. Existing geometric methods frequently lack closed-form operations or depend on arbitrary region ordering, limiting scalability. We introduce a scalable geometric framework with two components: (i) the Off-log metric, a smooth transformation mapping correlation matrices to symmetric zero-diagonal matrices. This enables closed-form expressions for distances, Frechet means, and linear models, allowing standard statistical modeling without complex manifold optimization. (ii) Grassmannian subspace discrimination, which compares subjects via principal-angle distances between eigenvector subspaces, resolving inherent sign and basis ambiguities. Both components integrate into standard machine-learning workflows for inference, regression, and classification. Validated across two clinical cohorts (Parkinson's and psychosis) and three ageing fMRI datasets, the Off-log metric increased sensitivity in permutation tests and matched or exceeded Riemannian and Euclidean baselines in classification. Brain-age prediction performance was comparable, with Riemannian metrics excelling in two of three cohorts. The Grassmannian method consistently outperformed Euclidean baselines, highlighting disease-relevant networks. Overall, geometry-aware representations improve sensitivity and predictive performance while remaining straightforward to deploy at scale.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Causal Orderings for In-Context Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22335)
*Sascha Xu,Sarah Mameche,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabOrder是一种融合因果结构发现与表格预测的模型，通过因果顺序约束注意力机制，在无监督方式下学习变量拓扑排序，提升了分布偏移和干预下的预测与插补性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格数据上下文学习主要依赖相关结构，在分布偏移或干预下不可靠；而因果发现方法虽能识别因果结构，却与预测架构脱节。本文旨在弥合这两类方法，同时推断并强制因果结构用于表格预测。

Method: 提出TabOrder模型，采用因果顺序约束的注意力机制，仅基于目标变量在学到的因果顺序中的前驱特征进行预测；通过基于似然的无监督目标学习最优变量排序，无需因果顺序标签；同时研究了数据缺失与因果方向识别的交互。

Result: 实验证实TabOrder能恢复准确的变量排序，有效完成预测和插补任务，并在真实生物数据的干预场景中提供了有意义的洞察。

Conclusion: TabOrder成功将因果结构推断与表格预测统一在一个框架内，在标准函数模型下具有理论合理性，并在分布偏移和干预下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In-context learning for tabular data sets strong predictive standards in observational settings; it however primarily relies on correlational structure, which becomes unreliable under distribution shift or intervention. While established methods to discover causal structure exist, they are often focused on structure identifiability and decoupled from the predictive architectures that could benefit from them.
  To bridge these perspectives, we study how to simultaneously infer and enforce causal structure in the form of topological variable orderings into tabular prediction. Unlike standard architectures, our model TabOrder uses causal order-constrained attention, basing predictions only on features that precede a target under a learned causal order. Similar to causal discovery methods, TabOrder learns the optimal variable ordering in an unsupervised manner through a likelihood-based objective. We justify this choice under standard functional model classes and also study how sample missingness, a common challenge in tabular data, interacts with causal direction identification. Empirically, we confirm that TabOrder recovers accurate variable orderings while addressing prediction and imputation tasks, as well as gives insight into real-world biological data under intervention.

</details>


### [118] [Physics-Informed Generative Solver: Bridging Data-Driven Priors and Conservation Laws for Stable Spatiotemporal Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22338)
*Ziyuan Zhu,Keyu Hu,Zhifei Chen,Yuhao Shi,Ming Bao,Jing Zhao,Gang Wang,Haitan Xu,Jiadong Li,Qijun Zhao,Xiaodong Li,Minghui Lu,Yanfeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的生成式求解器，通过Martingale正则化分数匹配和物理信息隐式分数采样，将稳定先验学习与推理时守恒律强制执行分离，在声学和气象学稀疏重构任务中实现了无重训练的物理一致性生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的生成模型在从稀疏测量重构连续物理场时，往往产生违反控制方程（如守恒律）的状态，缺乏物理一致性保证。

Method: 1) Martingale正则化分数匹配：在分数预训练中加入Score Fokker-Planck约束，获得动态稳定的先验；2) 物理信息隐式分数采样：在去噪轨迹中引入物理残差的梯度指导，将样本投影到可容许流形，无需重训练。

Result: 在声学中协同生成压力和粒子速度，实现抑制空间混叠的密集虚拟阵列；在极端稀疏的ERA5气象场中同样有效。

Conclusion: 建立了一种严格且可泛化的高维逆问题求解范式，弥合了生成式AI与第一性原理科学之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous physical fields from sparse measurements is a central inverse problem, but data-driven generative models can produce states that violate governing dynamics. We introduce a physics-informed generative solver that separates stable prior learning from inference-time enforcement of conservation laws. Martingale-Regularized Score Matching regularizes score pretraining with a Score Fokker-Planck constraint, yielding a dynamically stable prior. Physics-Informed Implicit Score Sampling then guides denoising trajectories by gradients of physical residuals, projecting samples toward admissible manifolds without retraining. In acoustics, the method co-generates pressure and particle velocity from sparse sensors, enabling dense virtual arrays that suppress spatial aliasing. The same framework generalizes to real-world ERA5 meteorological fields under extreme sparsity. Together, this work establishes a rigorous and generalizable paradigm for solving high-dimensional inverse problems, bridging the gap between generative artificial intelligence and first-principles science.

</details>


### [119] [From Snapshots to Trajectories: Learning Single-Cell Gene Expression Dynamics via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22340)
*Siyu Pu,Qingqing Long,Xiaohan Huang,Haotian Chen,Jiajia Wang,Meng Xiao,Xiao Luo,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou,Xuezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出scFM（单细胞流匹配）框架，通过耦合条件流匹配解决时间解析scRNA-seq数据中未配对快照导致的局部过渡模糊和长期预测漂移问题，在真实数据集上实现更优的时间插值与外推性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scRNA-seq轨迹推断方法（最优传输对齐和连续时间生成模型）面临两大挑战：(i) 未配对快照使得相邻时间点间的局部过渡不明确，导致监督不稳定；(ii) 长期预测依赖重复积分，建模误差累积引发分布漂移。

Method: 提出scFM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 计算相邻快照间的熵正则化OT耦合，构建软加权流匹配目标学习时变速度场；(2) 学习双向速度场并利用其一致性细化耦合，增强稀疏监督下的时间连贯性；(3) 引入分布级对齐和潜在动态正则化锚定长程 rollout，缓解漂移。

Result: 在真实时间序列scRNA-seq数据集上，scFM在时间插值和外推的分布预测性能上持续优于现有方法，轨迹重建更准确，中间缺失时间点的时间可视化更连贯。

Conclusion: scFM通过耦合条件流匹配有效解决了未配对快照下的局部过渡模糊和长期预测漂移问题，更忠实地恢复了潜在的时间基因表达动态。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) provides high-dimensional profiles of cellular states, enabling data-driven modeling of cellular dynamics over time. In practice, time-resolved scRNA-seq is collected at only a few discrete time points as unpaired snapshot populations, leaving substantial temporal gaps. This motivates trajectory inference at unmeasured time points. Existing methods mainly follow two directions, optimal-transport (OT) alignment provides distribution-level matching between observed snapshots, while continuous-time generative models support forecasting via learned dynamics. However, two challenges remain: (i) unpaired snapshots render local transitions between adjacent time points ambiguous, leading to unstable supervision; and (ii) long-horizon prediction relies on repeated integration, where small modeling errors compound and cause distribution drift. To address these challenges, we propose single-cell Flow Matching (scFM), a latent generative framework based on coupling-conditioned flow matching. First, we compute entropically regularized OT couplings between adjacent snapshots and use them to construct soft, weighted flow-matching targets for learning time-dependent velocity fields. Second, we learn bidirectional velocity fields and leverage their consistency to refine couplings and improve temporal coherence under sparse supervision. Third, we introduce distribution-level alignment and latent dynamic regularization to anchor long rollouts and mitigate drift. Experiments on real-world time-series scRNA-seq datasets show that scFM consistently improves distributional prediction performance for both temporal interpolation and extrapolation. Moreover, scFM yields more accurate trajectory reconstruction and temporally coherent visualizations where intermediate time points are absent, indicating a more faithful recovery of underlying temporal gene expression dynamics.

</details>


### [120] [A Boundary-Layer Mechanism for One-Third Scaling in Online Softmax Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22341)
*Marcel Kühn,Yoon Thelge,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了硬标签分类中平滑代理损失（如softmax交叉熵）与离散标签之间的不匹配，在在线师生模型中产生幂律学习曲线的渐近机制。通过中心化变量分析，发现后期仅边界层样本活跃，导致测试损失和泛化误差以α^{-1/3}幂律衰减，远慢于贝叶斯最优的α^{-1}。学习率调度可将误差改善至α^{-1/2}幂律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释硬标签分类中平滑代理损失与离散标签不匹配为何会导致幂律学习曲线，揭示其渐近机制。

Method: 在在线师生模型中，减去均值logit后分析中心化变量的热力学极限动力学；考察边界层宽度与噪声维持的学生方差Δ的关系；推导测试损失与泛化误差的幂律标度；并通过仿真和可控实验验证。

Result: 后期解给出α^{-1/3}幂律（测试损失与泛化误差），远慢于贝叶斯最优的α^{-1}；学习率调度可改进至α^{-1/2}。数据结构主导暂态过程，本机制是谱解释的互补而非替代。

Conclusion: 硬标签分类中平滑代理损失与离散标签的不匹配是产生幂律缩放律的渐近机制之一，但并非谱解释的替代，两者互补。

Abstract: Hard-label classification is usually trained with smooth surrogate losses, most prominently softmax cross-entropy. We isolate an asymptotic mechanism by which this mismatch between smooth surrogate and discrete labels produces power-law learning curves in an online teacher-student model. After subtracting the mean logit, the thermodynamic-limit dynamics close in centered variables: a growing centered student-teacher alignment $D$ and the residual student variance $Δ$. At late times, examples away from teacher decision boundaries are already classified confidently and contribute exponentially little. Only boundary layers of width $O(D^{-1})$ remain active, while the noise of fixed-learning-rate online gradient descent maintains a nonzero $Δ$. As a function of the training time $α$ the late-time solution yields a $α^{-1/3}$ power law not only for the test loss but also for the generalization error $ε_g$, i.e., one minus test accuracy. This is much slower than the $α^{-1}$ Bayes-optimal reference for the same model. We further show that learning-rate schedules can improve the generalization error towards a $ε_g \sim α^{-1/2}$ power law. Simulations support the predicted order parameter dynamics and learning curves. Controlled experiments with correlated Gaussian inputs and whitened pretrained features show that data structure can dominate transients. Therefore, our result is an asymptotic, complementary mechanism rather than an alternative to spectral explanations of neural scaling laws.

</details>


### [121] [Partial Fusion of Neural Networks: Efficient Tradeoffs Between Ensembles and Weight Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22350)
*Fabian Morelli,Stephan Eckstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经网络部分融合方法，通过基于神经元相似性的部分最优传输匹配，在集成模型与权重聚合之间灵活权衡计算成本与性能，并推广为集成模型的广义剪枝，也可应用于单网络实现类似效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络集成性能优越但计算成本高，权重聚合成本低但精度下降，现有方法无法灵活平衡计算成本与性能。

Method: 通过部分最优 transport 联合识别并匹配最相似的神经元，仅聚合相似神经元的权重，实现部分融合；并从广义剪枝视角统一理解该方法，允许神经元被删除或线性组合。

Result: 部分融合能够在计算成本与性能之间实现灵活权衡；广义剪枝应用于单网络时同样能带来类似效益。

Conclusion: 部分融合是一种有效的模型融合策略，通过控制融合程度可灵活调节效率与精度，且思想可推广至单网络剪枝场景。

Abstract: Ensembles of neural networks typically outperform individual networks but incur large computational costs, whereas weight aggregation produces less costly, yet also less accurate, aggregate models. We introduce partial fusion of networks, which interpolates between ensembles and weight aggregation and thus allows for a flexible tradeoff between computational cost and performance. A direct way to achieve this is to extend existing weight aggregation methods based on neuron-level similarity between different networks, where partial fusion then only aggregates weights of neurons which are most similar. We showcase one particular method to jointly identify which neurons are most similar and match them via partial optimal transport. Further, we consider the more general perspective of weight aggregation and partial fusion as generalized pruning of ensemble models, where neurons cannot just be deleted, but also linearly combined. Finally, we show that generalized pruning applied to a single network yields similar benefits as partial fusion by allowing for a tradeoff between isolating, deleting, and linearly combining neurons based on similarity. Our code is available at https://github.com/Fabian-Mor/partial_fusion_nn.

</details>


### [122] [VeriScale: Adversarial Test-Suite Scaling for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22368)
*Yifan Bai,Xiaoyang Liu,Zihao Mou,Guihong Wang,Jian Yu,Shuhan Xie,Yantao Li,Yangyu Zhang,Jingwei Liang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriScale是一个通过对抗性实现驱动的测试套件扩展与精简框架，用于构建更具区分力的代码生成与形式化规约生成基准。在Verina基础上构建的VerinaPlus将测试用例扩展83倍，VerinaLite在降低14倍成本的同时保持区分能力，揭示了现有LLM在代码正确性与可验证性方面的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准受限于正负测试用例的数量与质量，导致对LLM生成规约与实现的能力评估过于乐观，缺乏能够同时衡量功能正确性与形式化可验证性的高质量基准。

Method: 提出VeriScale框架，包含两个阶段：1) 测试套件扩展——通过对抗性实现构建多样且具有挑战性的测试用例；2) 测试套件精简——将扩展后的测试用例蒸馏为紧凑且具区分力的子集。在Verina上实例化得到VerinaPlus（扩展83倍）和VerinaLite（精简14倍）。

Result: 在8个先进LLM上的实验表明：VerinaPlus揭示了原始基准隐藏的模型缺陷，在SpecGen和CodeGen任务上分数大幅下降；VerinaLite在显著降低评估成本的同时保持了相当的区分能力。

Conclusion: 对抗性测试用例扩展能有效暴露LLM在代码生成与规约生成中的真实能力缺陷，且通过合理精简可以在评估效率与区分力之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for software engineering, constructing high-quality benchmarks is crucial for evaluating not just the functional correctness, but also the formal verifiability of generated code. However, existing benchmarks are limited by the quantity and quality of positive and negative test cases, leading to an overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations. To address this, we propose VeriScale, a novel framework driven by the adversarial implementations. It consists of two stages: test-suite expansion to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and test-suite reduction to distill them into compact yet discriminative suites. While VeriScale is general, we instantiate it on Verina to construct VerinaPlus, which expands the original test suites by over 83$\times$, and VerinaLite, a lightweight 14$\times$ variant. Our experiments across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that VerinaPlus exposes substantial model weaknesses hidden by the original benchmark, evidenced by sharp score drops on both SpecGen and CodeGen tasks, whereas VerinaLite maintains this discriminative power at a fraction of the evaluation cost. The enhanced benchmarks and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/XiaoyangLiu-sjtu/VeriScale.

</details>


### [123] [ASAP: Attention Sink Anchored Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22372)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Hanyoung Kim,Yanggee Kim,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASAP提出了一种无需训练的令牌剪枝框架，通过将注意力集中现象（attention sink）作为特征，利用懒随机游走建模信息流，基于扩散距离进行径向聚类与过渡权重池化，在图像、视频和视觉语言任务上实现最高48%的吞吐加速，同时保持甚至超越基准精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在高分辨率下因自注意力二次复杂度面临严重计算瓶颈，现有令牌缩减方法依赖局部指标（如单层注意力分数）易受注意力集中现象影响，导致无信息令牌被错误保留而显著前景令牌被剪除。

Method: 将ViT信息流建模为懒随机游走，识别注意力集中为概率质量的主导累加器；在累积转移矩阵内计算到集中点的扩散距离，通过径向扩散聚类分割令牌，再以过渡权重池化一次性压缩背景冗余。

Result: 在图像、视频和视觉语言任务上，ASAP超越现有最先进方法，实现最高48%的吞吐加速，同时保持甚至超越基线准确率。

Conclusion: ASAP通过重新定义注意力集中现象为可用特征，提出了一种无需训练、高效且保持精度的令牌剪枝方案，有效解决了ViT在高分辨率下的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) face severe computational bottlenecks due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention at high resolutions. Existing token reduction methods rely on local metrics - such as single-layer attention scores - that are inherently vulnerable to the attention sink phenomenon, where uninformative tokens are paradoxically preserved over salient foreground objects. We propose ASAP (Attention Sink Anchored Pruning), a training-free framework that recasts this sink as a feature. Modeling ViT information flow as a Lazy Random Walk, ASAP identifies the sink as a dominant accumulator of probability mass. By computing the diffusion distance to the sink within the cumulative transition matrix, ASAP partitions tokens via Radial Diffusion Clustering and compresses background redundancy through Transition Weight Pooling in a single shot. Extensive experiments across image, video, and vision-language tasks demonstrate ASAP outperforms state-of-the-art methods, accelerating throughput by up to 48% while maintaining - or even exceeding - baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [124] [Boundary-targeted Membership Inference Attacks on Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22373)
*Anthony Hughes,Alexander Goldberg,Prince Jha,Adam Perer,Nikolaos Aletras,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了边界目标选择策略（boundary-targeted selection），通过识别分类器低置信度的样本，显著提升了成员推断攻击（MIA）对安全分类器的有效性。实验表明，该方法可以在5%假阳性率下恢复19%被标记为情感支持需求的对话，比现有最先进MIA方法高出3.5倍，并发现基于内容的过滤无法防御，但噪声策略可有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分类器在生成式AI系统中至关重要，但它们训练于包含自残、心理健康讨论等敏感数据。这些模型的隐私风险（如成员推断攻击）尚未被充分理解。作者假设分类器低置信度的样本（即边界样本）更依赖记忆而非泛化，从而泄露成员信息，因此需要研究如何利用这一弱点进行攻击并探索防御策略。

Method: 提出边界目标选择策略（boundary-targeted selection），识别分类器在训练过程中低置信度的样本。这些样本位于决策边界附近，代表局部泛化失败，模型更依赖记忆解决歧义。通过针对性选择这些样本，放大成员推断信号，从而提升MIA攻击效果。

Result: 在针对情感支持检测的安全分类器上，使用边界目标选择策略，攻击者在5%假阳性率下能恢复19%被分类器标记为存在情感困扰的对话。相比仅使用最先进MIA方法，攻击效率提升了3.5倍。

Conclusion: 边界样本对成员推断攻击高度脆弱，基于内容的过滤无法有效防御此类攻击，但现有的噪声策略（如差分隐私）可以减轻边界样本的易感性。该发现对训练安全分类器的隐私保护具有重要启示。

Abstract: Safety classifiers are essential safeguards within generative AI systems, filtering harmful content or identifying at-risk users when interacting with large language models. Despite their necessity, these models are trained on sensitive datasets including discussions of self-harm and mental health, raising important, yet poorly understood, privacy concerns. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) allow adversaries to infer membership of examples used to train models. In this work, we hypothesize that identifying the examples on which the classifier is least confident are informative for an adversary to infer membership. This reflects a localized failure of generalization, where the model relies on memorization to resolve ambiguity in the training set. To investigate this, we introduce a new boundary-targeted selection strategy that identifies low confidence examples that amplify the signal of an examples membership within a training set. Our experimental results show that an adversary can recover 19\% of the conversations a safety classifier flagged as indicating user distress, at a 5\% false-positive rate, on a classifier fine-tuned for detecting a user who may require emotional support. This is $3.5$ times more than attacking using state-of-the-art MIA methods alone. Finally, we characterize the boundary laying examples and show that content-based filtering is ineffective for protection, and existing noise strategies can effectively mitigate susceptibility of these examples.

</details>


### [125] [Don't Forget the Critic: Value-Based Data Rehearsal for Multi-Cyclic Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22454)
*Benjamin Poole,Andrew Quinn,Li Yang,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多循环持续强化学习场景下，基于值函数的Q值正则化数据重演方法，提出Qreg+NWLU，通过连续数据重演和“无等待”正则化改进，在值函数逼近框架下提升了学习效率、缓解了灾难性遗忘并促进了知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习中的数据重演方法局限于策略梯度框架，仅对演员进行正则化而忽略了评论家正则化，且缺乏对任务序列重复的多循环环境（加剧遗忘与可塑性下降）的评估。

Method: 提出Qreg+NWLU方法，包含两个创新：(1) 连续数据重演——在训练过程中动态收集和更新存储的Q值；(2)“无等待”正则化——正则化从训练开始立即应用而非等待第一个任务结束后。

Result: 在值函数逼近框架下，Qreg+NWLU相比Qreg和传统CRL方法在学习效率、遗忘缓解和知识迁移方面均取得改进。

Conclusion: 数据重演在值函数逼近框架中同样有效，连续数据重演与即时正则化策略能够显著提升多循环持续强化学习的性能。

Abstract: Data rehearsal has emerged as a leading approach for mitigating catastrophic forgetting in Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL). However, existing work remains confined to policy gradient frameworks, regularizing only actors due to the performance degradation incurred by critic regularization. This actor-centric approach overlooks the potential of data rehearsal for value function approximation. Moreover, existing evaluations in CRL rarely consider multi-cyclic environments where task sequences repeat, a critical real-world scenario that exacerbates forgetting and plasticity. We investigate data rehearsal for Deep Q-Networks using Q-value regularization in multi-cyclic settings and propose Qreg+NWLU which introduces two simple modifications: (1) continuous data rehearsal that dynamically collects and updates stored Q-values throughout training, and (2) "No-Wait" regularization that applies immediately rather than after the first task. Together, these modifications yield improvements in learning efficiency, forgetting mitigation, and knowledge transfer over Qreg and conventional CRL methods within value function approximation settings.

</details>


### [126] [Target-Aligned Bellman Backup for Cross-domain Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22376)
*Wei Liu,Ting Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对跨域离线强化学习中基于过渡相似性评估源域数据可迁移性可能导致长期回报不一致的问题，本文提出Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB)方法，通过衡量源域数据对目标域Bellman目标估计的贡献来选择性地利用数据，在目标域数据高度受限的场景下取得了一致优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域离线强化学习方法通过评估源域数据与目标域过渡的相似性来隐式进行过渡级选择，但过渡级相似性并不保证长期回报的一致性，相似过渡在目标域中可能导致截然不同的结果，从而误导策略学习。

Method: 提出Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB)方法，从策略学习的基本目标出发，基于源域过渡与目标域Bellman目标的对齐程度（而非表面过渡相似性）来评估其可迁移性，通过衡量源域数据对目标域准确Bellman目标估计的贡献来选择性地利用数据。

Result: 在多种跨域离线RL场景（目标域数据高度受限）下的实验结果表明，TABB方法持续取得了优异的性能。

Conclusion: 基于Bellman目标对齐的数据选择策略比基于过渡相似性的方法更能有效提升跨域离线强化学习中策略学习的性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning (CDRL) aims to improve policy learning in a target domain by leveraging data collected from a source domain. Existing works typically assess the transferability of source-domain data by measuring its similarity to target-domain transitions, and implicitly perform transition-level selection. Transitions that are considered similar are assigned higher weights or rewards, while dissimilar ones are down-weighted. However, transition-level similarity does not necessarily imply consistency in long-term returns. Even visually or dynamically similar transitions may lead to significantly different outcomes in the target domain, which can mislead policy learning and degrade performance. To address this issue, we revisit the fundamental objective of policy learning. Since policy optimization ultimately relies on Bellman targets to evaluate the quality of decisions, we propose to assess the transferability of source-domain transitions based on their alignment with target-domain Bellman targets, rather than superficial transition similarity. Based on this insight, we propose a method termed Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB), which selectively leverages source-domain data by measuring their contribution to accurate Bellman target estimation in the target domain. We evaluate TABB across a broad range of cross-domain offline RL settings with highly limited target-domain data. Experimental results show that TABB consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [127] [BioFormer: Rethinking Cross-Subject Generalization via Spectral Structural Alignment in Biomedical Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22468)
*Guikang Du,Haoran Li,Xinyu Liu,Zhibo Zhang,Xiaoli Gong,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioFormer通过频谱漂移视角显式建模个体差异，利用频带对齐模块和样本条件层归一化抑制生物医学时间序列中的跨主体变异性，在6个数据集上F1分数提升6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法隐式抑制主体特异性变异性，缺乏显式建模，作者引入频谱漂移（spectral drift）作为新视角来刻画这一问题。

Method: 提出BioFormer，核心包括：(1) 频带对齐模块(FBAM)，从频谱分布生成逐带调制因子，自适应调整幅度和相位以对齐频谱结构；(2) 样本条件层归一化，从信号内在统计量而非主体身份推断归一化参数，稳定跨主体表征。

Result: 在6个数据集上超越12个基线方法，绝对F1分数提升6%。

Conclusion: 显式建模频谱漂移能有效抑制主体特异性变异性，提升跨主体泛化能力。

Abstract: Cross-subject generalization in biomedical time-series refers to training on data from some subjects and testing on unseen subjects.The key challenge is to suppress subject specific variability in BTS representations.Most existing methods implicitly suppress the variability through model building or subject adversarial learning, but rarely model it explicitly.We introduce spectral drift as a new perspective to characterize subject specific variability.Specifically, BTS signals under the same label often share consistent oscillatory structure, yet exhibit subject-dependent magnitude or phase shifts in specific frequency components, which we interpret as subject-specific variability. Building on this insight, we propose BioFormer.At its core is a Frequency-Band Alignment Module(FBAM) that generates band-wise modulation factors from the spectral distribution and adaptively adjusts amplitude and phase to align spectral structure, thereby mitigating variability.We further pair FBAM with Sample Conditional Layer Normalization, which infers normalization parameters from intrinsic signal statistics rather than subject identity, stabilizing cross-subject representations.Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate that BioFormer outperforms 12 baselines, yielding absolute F1-score improvements of 6%.

</details>


### [128] [Towards Explainability of SLMs by investigating Token Level Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22377)
*Sayantani Ghosh,Rajashik Datta,Amit Kumar Das,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的激活流网络（AFN）框架，通过计算BERT第8层隐藏状态的L2范数来衡量token级别的表征重要性，并引入基于阈值的激活桶划分方法，发现语义核心词占据高激活桶，而结构辅助词贡献较小。实验表明第8层是语义整合的关键区域，提供了一种比注意力分析更高效、可解释的模型透明度提升方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的可解释性方法常强调结构重要但语义薄弱的token（如标点符号），而非有意义的语义关系，导致对BERT等大模型内部机制的理解仍然不透明。作者希望构建一种轻量级、模型无关的框架，从激活强度角度直接量化token的语义重要性，推动BERT从"黑箱"向"玻璃箱"转变。

Method: 提出激活流网络（AFN）框架，具体包括：(1) 基于第8层隐藏状态L2范数计算Token激活强度（Token Activation Strength）；(2) 引入基于经验上四分位数边界的阈值激活桶划分，将token分为高激活组和低激活组；(3) 通过比较两组token的语义贡献差异来验证框架有效性。

Result: 实验观察到语义有意义的实词始终占据高激活桶，并主导表征激活变化，而结构辅助性token贡献较小；第8层被识别为语义整合的关键区域，平衡了结构信息和语义信息的处理。

Conclusion: 激活强度集中在语义信息丰富的token周围，该方法提供了一种可解释且计算高效的注意力分析替代方案，有助于将BERT从"黑箱"转变为更透明的"玻璃箱"模型。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models such as BERT having 110M+ parameters have revolutionized natural language understanding, yet their internal mechanisms remain largely opaque to researchers and practitioners. Traditional attention-based interpretability methods often emphasize structurally important but semantically weak tokens such as punctuation marks rather than meaningful semantic relationships. This work introduces a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for quantifying token-level representational importance using hidden-state activation strengths at Layer 8 of BERT. The proposed Activation Flow Network (AFN) framework computes Token Activation Strength using the L2 norm of Layer-8 hidden representations, enabling direct ranking of semantically salient tokens. The study further introduces a threshold-based activation bucket formulation that partitions tokens into HIGH-activation and LOW-activation groups using an empirical upper-quartile activation boundary. Experimental observations demonstrate that semantically meaningful content words consistently occupy the HIGH-activation bucket and dominate representational activation shifts, while structurally supportive tokens contribute comparatively less. The results suggest that Layer 8 acts as a critical semantic consolidation zone balancing structural and semantic information processing. By revealing how activation magnitudes concentrate around semantically informative tokens, this work provides an interpretable and computationally efficient alternative to attentioncentric analysis, contributing toward transforming BERT from a "black box" into a more transparent "glass box" model for natural language understanding.

</details>


### [129] [Implicit Regularization of Mini-Batch Training in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22480)
*Clement Wang,Antoine Vialle,Robin Vaysse,Thomas Bonald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明随机节点采样（RNS）在8/10数据集上匹配或超越全图训练，并通过后向误差分析揭示其隐式正则化机制：RNS采样子图的期望损失更接近全图损失，且梯度方差更低，从而产生更好的隐式优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）的小批量训练因子图采样改变拓扑并引入边界效应，现有工作致力于开发结构感知采样器。本文旨在重新审视该问题，探究最简单的随机节点采样方案是否有效，并从理论角度解释其行为。

Method: 对图小批量随机梯度下降（SGD）进行后向误差分析（backward error analysis），证明其隐式最小化采样损失加上一个与梯度方差成正比的正则化项。通过理论分析和实验对比，揭示RNS虽然丢弃局部结构，但其产生的期望损失更接近全图损失，且每批梯度方差更低。

Result: RNS在8/10个数据集上匹配或超越全图训练效果，同时显著降低计算时间和内存消耗。理论分析表明，RNS的隐式正则化目标优于其他复杂采样器。

Conclusion: 图采样器的选择本质上是一种隐式正则化（implicit regularization）形式，RNS是一种强健且具有理论依据的可扩展GNN训练方法，为未来设计图采样策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: Mini-batch training of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) is fundamentally different from training on i.i.d. data: sampling a subgraph alters the topology and introduces boundary effects, leading prior work to develop structure-aware samplers that preserve local connectivity and reduce embedding variance. Surprisingly, we demonstrate that the simplest possible scheme, Random Node Sampling (RNS), training on the induced subgraph of uniformly sampled nodes, matches or outperforms full-graph training on 8 of 10 datasets at a fraction of the wall-clock time and memory. To explain this, we apply backward error analysis to graph mini-batch Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) and show that it implicitly minimizes the sampled loss plus a regularizer proportional to the mini-batch gradient variance, a quantity directly shaped by the sampler. Although RNS discards local structure, it produces mini-batches whose expected loss is closer to the full-graph loss, and whose per-batch gradients have lower variance, yielding a better implicit objective. Our analysis reframes the choice of graph sampler as a form of implicit regularization, and identifies RNS as a strong, theoretically grounded method for scalable GNN training.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient Higher-order Subgraph Attribution via Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22385)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Grégoire Montavon,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性时间算法，通过消息传递技术利用分配律，将GNN-LRP的指数级子图归因复杂度降至线性，并推广到考虑邻域特征的子图归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN-LRP通过计算层间游走的相关性归因来阐释GNN，但其子图归因需要对指数级数量的游走路径求和，计算复杂度过高，限制了其可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 利用消息传递技术和分配律（distributive property），提出线性时间（相对于网络深度）的算法，直接计算高阶解释的归因量，避免显式枚举所有游走路径。同时将算法推广到考虑邻域图特征的一般化子图归因。

Result: 实验表明所提算法显著加速了GNN-LRP的计算，且提出的广义子图归因方法具有高实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过利用分配律和消息传递，成功将GNN-LRP子图归因从指数复杂度降至线性复杂度，并扩展了归因能力，为GNN高阶解释方法的高效计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Explaining graph neural networks (GNNs) has become more and more important recently. Higher-order interpretation schemes, such as GNN-LRP (layer-wise relevance propagation for GNN), emerged as powerful tools for unraveling how different features interact thereby contributing to explaining GNNs. GNN-LRP gives a relevance attribution of walks between nodes at each layer, and the subgraph attribution is expressed as a sum over exponentially many such walks. In this work, we demonstrate that such exponential complexity can be avoided. In particular, we propose novel algorithms that enable to attribute subgraphs with GNN-LRP in linear-time (w.r.t. the network depth). Our algorithms are derived via message passing techniques that make use of the distributive property, thereby directly computing quantities for higher-order explanations. We further adapt our efficient algorithms to compute a generalization of subgraph attributions that also takes into account the neighboring graph features. Experimental results show the significant acceleration of the proposed algorithms and demonstrate the high usefulness and scalability of our novel generalized subgraph attribution method.

</details>


### [131] [Understanding Multimodal Failure in Action-Chunking Behavioral Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22493)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Massimiliano Datres,Ariel Rodriguez,Sebastian Bodenstedt,Gitta Kutyniok,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了行为克隆中观测对应多有效动作的问题，研究了动作分块策略下不同多模态参数化的失败机制，包括潜变量策略中后验-先验正则化的权衡，以及动作空间生成策略中Lipschitz常数对多模态覆盖的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆中同一观测对应多个有效动作（多模态性）导致学习困难，现有方法对此问题理解不足。

Method: 通过理论分析潜变量策略和动作空间生成策略两种多模态参数化方案，研究后验-先验正则化、Lipschitz常数等关键因素对多模态行为建模的影响。

Result: 发现了两种参数化方案的不同失败模式：潜变量策略中正则化强度影响模式信息保持；动作空间生成策略中Lipschitz常数限制了对多模态的覆盖能力。

Conclusion: 在合成多模态任务和机器人仿真基准实验验证了上述机制，为改进多模态行为克隆提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning becomes difficult when the same observation admits several valid actions. We study this problem for action-chunking policies and show that different multimodal parameterizations fail in different ways. For latent-variable policies, posterior-prior regularization makes deployment-time sampling more reliable, but excessive regularization removes the action-conditioned information needed to distinguish demonstrated modes. Reducing this regularization can preserve mode information, but then success depends on whether the prior covers the relevant latent regions. For action-space generative policies, multimodality is constrained by the smoothness of the base-to-action transport: a map with small Lipschitz constant cannot assign substantial probability to many well-separated modes. Covering many modes therefore requires either sharp transitions in base space or off-support bridge regions in action space. Experiments on synthetic multimodal tasks and robotic simulation benchmarks support these mechanisms.

</details>


### [132] [Hybrid Kolmogorov-Arnold Network and XGBoost Framework for Week-Ahead Price Forecasting in Australia's National Electricity Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22387)
*Houxuan Zhou,Sriram Prasad,Chenghao Huang,Jiajie Feng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合KAN与XGBoost的混合框架用于澳大利亚国家电力市场（NEM）的周前电价预测，在真实数据上相比基线方法（SARIMAX、LSTM、独立KAN和XGBoost）实现了约12%的MAE降低，验证了混合学习策略在高动态电力市场中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测因强波动性、非线性动力学和频繁极端价格尖峰而极具挑战性，尤其在澳大利亚NEM这类高可再生能源渗透率的市场中不确定性更高，现有方法难以同时捕获长期依赖和短期波动。

Method: 提出KAN+XGBoost混合框架，利用KAN的全局非线性表示能力与XGBoost的局部鲁棒性相结合，采用扩展窗口评估策略在真实NEM数据上进行实验。

Result: 该模型在所有基准方法中表现最优，相比XGBoost降低MAE约12%，相比朴素基线降低MAE超过50%。

Conclusion: 混合学习策略为高动态电力市场中的电价预测提供了有效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate electricity price forecasting (EPF) is essential for market participants to support operational planning and risk management, yet remains challenging due to strong volatility, nonlinear dynamics, and frequent extreme price spikes. These challenges are particularly pronounced in the Australian National Electricity Market (NEM), where high renewable penetration further increases uncertainty. This paper investigates week-ahead electricity price forecasting and proposes a hybrid KAN+XGBoost framework that integrates Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) with tree-based learning. The proposed approach combines the global nonlinear representation capability of KAN with the local robustness of XGBoost to capture both long-term dependencies and short-term price fluctuations. Experiments are conducted on real-world NEM data using an expanding window evaluation strategy. The results demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms benchmark methods, including SARIMAX, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), standalone KAN, and XGBoost, reducing MAE by approximately 12% compared to XGBoost and by over 50% compared to a naive baseline. The results suggest that hybrid learning strategies provide an effective and robust solution for electricity price forecasting in highly dynamic electricity markets.

</details>


### [133] [The Neural Compiler: Program-to-Network Translation for Hybrid Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22498)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Neural Compiler，一个将 Scheme 风格的一阶表达式语言程序编译为冻结且可微分的 PyTorch 模块的系统，能够精确编码已知物理方程，并与学习模块组合成混合模型，在六个实验领域中验证了其数值精度、参数效率及系统组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习需要在已知物理规律与从数据中学习的未知参数或修正项之间进行结合。现有方法要么忽略已知结构，要么将其编码为软惩罚项，要么需要为每个方程手写 PyTorch 代码，缺乏系统性、可组合的解决方案。

Method: 将一阶 Scheme 风格的表达式语言编写的程序编译为 Frozen PyTorch 模块（字符串输入、模块输出），模块与源程序达到浮点精度一致且通过 autograd 提供梯度，用于混合模型中精确编码已知物理并学习未知部分。支持 51 种基本运算，包括向量与矩阵代数，支持 PDE 离散化和混合科学模型。

Result: 在六个实验领域（Feynman 物理方程、Lotka-Volterra 动力学、阻尼摆、一维热方程、三维矢量力学、组合泛化）中，编译模块与手写 PyTorch 实现数值一致。仅用 1-4 个可训练参数即可在大多数情况下以小于 1% 的误差恢复物理常数，而 PINN 基线（超 8500 参数）误差为 7-93%。编译模块组合误差为零，而神经网络近似在深层组合链中累积大误差。

Conclusion: 编译器的主要价值不在于比手写方程更优的精度，而在于系统性可组合性：从符号规范生成正确、可微分的模块，无需为每个方程重写代码。该字符串输入、模块输出的接口也为大语言模型将科学描述翻译为可执行微分模块提供了自然的目标。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning often requires combining known physics with unknown parameters or correction terms learned from data. Existing approaches either ignore known structure, encode it as a soft penalty, or require hand-written PyTorch code for each equation. We present The Neural Compiler, a system that translates programs written in a first-order Scheme-like expression language into frozen, differentiable PyTorch modules. These modules match the source program to floating-point precision and provide gradients through autograd. In hybrid models, the compiled module encodes known physics exactly while learned components model the unknown remainder. We evaluate the compiler across six experiment domains: Feynman physics equations, Lotka-Volterra dynamics, a damped pendulum, a one-dimensional heat equation, three-dimensional vector mechanics, and compositional generalization. Compiled modules match hand-coded PyTorch implementations numerically for single equations, showing no accuracy loss from compilation. With only 1 to 4 trainable parameters, compiled models recover physical constants to less than 1 percent error in most cases, while standard PINN baselines with more than 8500 parameters show 7 to 93 percent error. Compiled modules also compose with zero error, while neural approximations can accumulate large errors in deep composition chains. The main value of the compiler is not improved accuracy over hand-coded equations, but systematic composability: it generates correct, differentiable modules from symbolic specifications without rewriting each equation by hand. The system supports 51 primitive operations, including vector and matrix algebra, enabling PDE discretizations and hybrid scientific models. This string-in, module-out interface also provides a natural target for large language models that translate scientific descriptions into executable differentiable modules.

</details>


### [134] [A Posterior-Predictive Variance Decomposition for Epistemic and Aleatoric Uncertainty in Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22390)
*Yinsong Chen,Samson S. Yu,Kashem M. Muttaqi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用全方差公式将异方差神经网络回归与贝叶斯后验近似中的总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性（AU）和认知不确定性（EU），并提出了包含合成实验、真实SCADA数据验证和数据集规模缩放实验的三模块评估框架，验证了分解的理论一致性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风电功率预测方法仅报告混合了偶然与认知来源的单一预测不确定性，缺乏可靠的解耦评估手段，无法有效指导模型在分布偏移等场景下的可信决策。

Method: 基于全方差公式，在异方差神经网络回归与贝叶斯后验近似联合框架下推导出总不确定性的显式分解为AU和EU；采用β-NLL训练调节均值-方差学习权衡；设计三模块评估框架（受控合成实验、真实SCADA数据属性驱动验证、数据集规模缩放实验）来验证解耦效果。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界实验中，分解出的AU和EU分量在噪声结构、分布偏移及训练规模变化下均呈现出理论上一致的变化方向，验证了分解框架的理论一致性和操作实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性分解与评估协议在理论上是一致的，在实际风电功率预测中具有操作价值，可为不确定性解耦提供无真实标签的验证标准。

Abstract: Accurate wind power forecasting requires reliable uncertainty quantification, yet most existing methods report a single predictive uncertainty that conflates epistemic and aleatoric sources. This paper applies the law of total variance to the joint setting of heteroscedastic neural network regression and Bayesian posterior approximation, deriving an explicit decomposition of total uncertainty (TU) into aleatoric (AU) and epistemic (EU) components. The resulting estimators are compatible with standard posterior-approximation methods and with $β$-NLL training to regulate the mean--variance learning trade-off. A wind power--specific evaluation framework is proposed to validate disentanglement without access to ground-truth uncertainty labels, comprising three modules: controlled synthetic experiments to verify responses to heteroscedastic noise and distribution shift; data-property--driven validation on a real-world wind turbine SCADA dataset; and dataset-size scaling experiments to examine the predicted asymptotic behavior of EU. Across synthetic and real-world experiments, the decomposed AU and EU components respond in theoretically consistent directions to noise structure, distributional shift, and training-scale variation, supporting the theoretical consistency and operational utility of the proposed decomposition and evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [135] [Stabilising Explainability Fragility in Cybersecurity AI: The Impact and Mitigation of Multicollinearity in Public Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22529)
*Ioannis J. Vourganas,Anna Lito Michala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI可解释性在入侵检测中的多重共线性导致的不稳定性问题，提出理论定理、脆弱性评分、两种缓解方法（CAA-Filtering和SHARP），并通过实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管后验可解释性工具（如SHAP、LIME）在入侵检测中被广泛使用，但特征相关性对解释鲁棒性的影响尚未被评估，存在可解释性不稳定的安全隐患。

Method: 提出理论定理证明多重共线性会放大归因方差；提出可解释性脆弱性评分；设计CAA-Filtering（分组归因稳定解释）和SHARP（训练时正则化框架）两种缓解方法；使用Kendall's τ量化不稳定性。

Result: 在UNSW-NB15基准数据集上，对线性、树、核方法、神经网络四类模型，在完整和剪枝特征集上验证了理论；所提方法可有效缓解多重共线性导致的解释不稳定性，且保持预测性能稳定。

Conclusion: 多重共线性导致的解释不稳定性是安全关键场景中XAI可信度和可复现性的重要威胁，应将多重共线性缓解纳入入侵检测流程，并提供了实践指南。

Abstract: This paper investigates a unexplored yet impactful vulnerability in AI explainability used in intrusion detection (IDS): multicollinearity-induced instability. Despite extensive reliance on post-hoc explainability tools such as SHAP or LIME, the impact of correlated features on explanation robustness is not evaluated. We introduce a formal theorem stating that multicollinearity inflates attribution variance. This demonstrates that explanations and feature importances are non-identifiable under multicollinearity. A suite of comprehensive experiments validates the theorem on a representative benchmark dataset, UNSW-NB15. Four widely used families of models are evaluated, including linear, tree-based, kernel, and neural, across full and pruned feature sets based on VIF and correlation thresholding. We propose the novel metric of Explanability Fragility Score and two novel methods to mitigate it with variable integration complexity. CAA-Filtering focuses on stabilising explanations by grouping attributions of trained models. SHARP is a novel training-time regularisation framework that penalises attribution instability, enabling controllable and monotonic improvement of explainability stability. The findings support stable predictive performance, using Kendall's τ to quantify instability across bootstrapped explanations. This work has direct implications for the trustworthiness and reproducibility of XAI in security-critical contexts, and motivates incorporating multicollinearity mitigations into the IDS pipelines, providing a set of guidelines for practitioners.

</details>


### [136] [Cross-Species RSA Reveals Conserved Early Visual Alignment but Divergent Higher-Area Rankings Across Human fMRI and Macaque Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22401)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将五种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP、随机基线）在相同模型权重下，分别与人类fMRI和猕猴电生理数据进行脑对齐比较。发现早期视觉皮层（V1/V2）的对齐跨物种一致（STDP和PC最佳），但高级皮层（IT）的对齐受模型容量和训练数据影响更大，且学习规则排序跨物种不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学习规则与脑对齐之间的关系是否跨物种泛化，即之前在人类V1上发现的无训练CNN与反向传播对齐效果相当的结果，是否在猕猴电生理数据上也能得到验证。

Method: 使用五种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP、随机权重基线）训练相同架构的CNN，采用表征相似性分析（RSA），分别在两个猕猴电生理数据集（MajajHong2015用于V4/IT，FreemanZiemba2013用于V1/V2）上进行脑对齐评估，并与人类fMRI结果对比。额外纳入预训练ResNet-50作为对照，报告噪声上限、多种子变异性（5个种子）和刺激控制分析。

Result: (1) 所有模型在猕猴早期视觉皮层的对齐度（rho=0.15-0.30）高于人类fMRI（rho=0.01-0.08）；(2) STDP和PC在猕猴V1/V2对齐最佳（rho~0.30和0.28），与人类V1结果一致；(3) IT层学习规则排序跨物种无显著相关（Kendall's tau=0.00, p=1.00）；(4) 预训练ResNet-50在猕猴IT层对齐度（rho=0.25）显著高于所有定制CNN条件（rho=0.07-0.14）。

Conclusion: 早期视觉对齐跨物种鲁棒，而高级皮层对齐受模型容量和刺激域调节，学习规则的作用在高级皮层被其他因素所掩盖。

Abstract: Does the relationship between learning rules and brain alignment generalize across species? We extend our prior finding that untrained CNNs match backpropagation at human V1 by testing the same five learning rules against macaque electrophysiology. The rules are backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), and an untrained random-weights baseline. The macaque data come from two datasets: MajajHong2015 (V4/IT, 3,200 stimulus presentations, 88/168 neurons) and FreemanZiemba2013 (V1/V2, 135 stimuli, 102/103 neurons). Using RSA with identical model weights from our human study, we find: (1) all models achieve higher alignment with macaque early visual cortex (rho = 0.15-0.30 at V1/V2) than with human fMRI (rho = 0.01-0.08), consistent with the higher signal-to-noise ratio of electrophysiology; (2) STDP and PC produce the highest macaque V1/V2 alignment (rho ~ 0.30 and 0.28), consistent with their leading position among trained rules in human V1; (3) at IT, learning rule rankings show no detectable correlation across species (Kendall's tau = 0.00, p = 1.00), though this null result is expected given that n = 5 provides power only at tau = +/-1.0, and is further confounded by stimulus set differences; (4) a pretrained ResNet-50 (ImageNet) achieves rho = 0.25 at macaque IT, substantially above all custom CNN conditions (rho = 0.07-0.14), suggesting IT alignment is limited by model capacity and training data rather than by the learning rule. Noise ceilings, multi-seed variability (5 seeds), and a stimulus-control analysis are reported. These results demonstrate that early visual alignment is robust across species, while higher-area alignment is modulated by model capacity and stimulus domain.

</details>


### [137] [MoSA: Motion-constrained Stress Adaptation for Mitigating Real-to-Sim Gap in Continuum Dynamics via Learning Residual Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22597)
*Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Jingkai Sun,Yi Gu,Yunyang Mo,Jiahang Cao,Qiang Zhang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoSA提出了一种运动约束的应力自适应框架，以各向同性模型为物理先验，通过学习残余应力算子捕获温和的各向异性和非均匀性，从而缩小真实到模拟的动力学差距，在精度、泛化性和鲁棒性上表现优异，并提升了机器人操作中的仿真到现实迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过标定物理参数来校准模拟器，但精度受限于假设材料均匀且各向同性的物理模型；虽然神经网络可以端到端拟合动力学，但黑箱建模丢弃了强物理先验，导致数据效率低且易过拟合。为了进一步缩小真实到模拟的差距，需要针对残余的各向异性和非均匀性效应进行建模。

Method: MoSA框架包含三个关键组件：（1）以各向同性模型作为物理先验，学习残余应力算子来捕获温和的各向异性和非均匀性；（2）通过微平面约束的再分配机制，在物理信息级联网络中逐步自适应应力；（3）通过监督变形场的时空导数施加运动约束。

Result: 实验表明，学习到的动力学在精度、泛化性和鲁棒性上优于现有方法，且学到的残余各向异性具有物理可解释性；在机器人操作场景中验证，更好的真实到模拟动力学建模转化为更可靠的模拟到现实迁移。

Conclusion: MoSA通过物理先验与数据驱动相结合的方式，有效捕获了真实材料中的残余各向异性和非均匀性效应，为缩小真实到模拟的动力学差距提供了一种有效框架，并在机器人操作任务中验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: Learning real-world dynamics from visual observations is crucial for various domains. A common strategy is to calibrate simulators by estimating physical parameters, yet accuracy is ultimately bounded by the underlying physical models, which often assume materials are homogeneous and isotropic. Even if reasonable, real-world objects typically exhibit mild anisotropy and heterogeneity. After the near-isotropic backbone is well calibrated, these residual effects become the key bottleneck for further closing the real-to-sim gap. Although neural networks can fit dynamics end-to-end, such black-box modeling discards strong physical priors, leading to poor data efficiency and overfitting. Therefore, we propose MoSA, a motion-constrained stress adaptation framework that targets these residual effects to further improve real-to-sim dynamics learning. MoSA uses an isotropic model as a physics prior and learns residual stress operators to capture mild anisotropy and heterogeneity. It progressively adapts stresses via microplane-constrained redistribution in a physics-informed cascaded network. We further impose motion constraints by supervising temporal and spatial derivatives of the deformation field. Experimentally, our learned dynamics achieves superior accuracy, generalization, and robustness, while learning physically meaningful residual anisotropy. Finally, we validate MoSA in a robot manipulation setting, showing that better real-to-sim dynamics modeling translates into more reliable sim-to-real transfer. Project Page is available at https://mercerai.github.io/MoSA/.

</details>


### [138] [Minimum Description Length based Granular-Ball Tree Regularization for Spectral Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22410)
*Zeqiang Xian,Caihui Liu,Yong Zhang,Wenjing Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小描述长度（MDL）的粒球树正则化谱聚类方法（MDL-GBTRSC），通过局部MDL模型选择构建粒球树，利用互惠邻域连续性保留可靠局部连接，并引入共享邻居桥代码调整弱局部桥接关系，将可解释的局部表示学习与亲和图构建统一到谱聚类框架中，在多个数据集上取得了最优的平均ARI和NMI指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于粒球的谱聚类方法通常使用粗粒度代表来降低图复杂度，但学习到的局部区域往往仅作为图节点或锚点，其结构信息未能充分用于正则化原始样本级图，导致局部连接可靠性不足且难以适应异质数据结构。

Method: 该方法首先通过局部MDL模型选择构建粒球树，利用互惠邻域连续性阻止破坏可靠局部连接的划分；从树中获得的稳定叶球提供编码尺度信息用于正则化样本级亲和图；同时引入共享邻居桥代码调整弱局部桥接关系，无需额外用户指定阈值。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的实验表明，在固定配置协议下，MDL-GBTRSC相比经典谱聚类基线方法以及代表性的粒球、微簇和锚点方法，取得了最优的平均ARI和NMI指标。

Conclusion: MDL-GBTRSC通过将可解释的局部表示学习与亲和图构建统一在谱聚类框架中，有效解决了现有方法对局部结构信息利用不足的问题，提升了聚类性能。

Abstract: Spectral clustering largely depends on the affinity graph, yet constructing a graph that preserves reliable local connectivity while adapting to heterogeneous data structures remains challenging. Existing granular-ball-based spectral clustering methods usually reduce graph complexity by using coarse-grained representatives. However, the learned local regions are often treated as graph nodes or anchors, and their structural information is not sufficiently used to regularize the original sample-level graph. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Minimum Description Length based Granular-Ball Tree-Regularized Spectral Clustering method, termed MDL-GBTRSC. The proposed method constructs a granular-ball tree through local MDL model selection, with reciprocal neighborhood continuity used to discourage splits that break reliable local connections. The stable leaf balls obtained from the tree provide coding-scale information for regularizing the sample-level affinity graph. In addition, a shared-neighbor bridge code is introduced to adjust weak local bridge relations without requiring an additional user-specified threshold. In this way, MDL-GBTRSC connects interpretable local representation learning with affinity graph construction in a unified spectral clustering framework. Experiments on real and synthetic datasets show that MDL-GBTRSC achieves the best average ARI and NMI under the adopted fixed-configuration protocol compared with classical spectral clustering baselines and representative granular-ball, micro-cluster, and anchor-based methods.

</details>


### [139] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于相对化选项（relativised options）的层次化框架，通过在离线目标条件强化学习中引入绝对抽象（absolute abstraction）和层级表征，实现跨相似状态-目标对的经验复用，并设计了两种简单算法，实验表明该归纳偏置显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDP在目标条件强化学习中因对称性和共享结构存在大量冗余，而现有层次化策略主要关注时间抽象以减少视野，忽略了层次化也能带来绝对抽象的优势，即复用跨相似上下文（状态-目标对）的经验。

Method: 引入相对化选项（relativised options）以及不同层级的不同表征，使智能体能从绝对参考系中抽象出来，实现跨相似上下文的经验复用；基于此框架设计了两种简单算法。

Result: 实验表明，这种归纳偏置（层次化带来的绝对抽象）在离线目标条件强化学习中显著提升了性能。

Conclusion: 层次化策略不仅具有时间抽象能力，还能实现绝对抽象，通过相对化选项和层级表征有效复用经验，提升离线GCRL的性能。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [140] [Asymmetric Virtual Memory Paging for Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22416)
*An Xuan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称虚拟内存分页（AVMP），针对混合语言模型（如Jamba）中注意力层KV缓存和SSM状态两种不同缓存类型的物理内存管理问题，通过统一虚拟地址空间下的分离物理池和动态容量迁移，实现了高达13.3倍的吞吐量提升和7.6%的OOM下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语言模型（如Jamba）同时包含注意力层（KV缓存随序列长度线性增长）和状态空间模型（SSM状态每层固定），现有推理引擎要么将SSM状态填充到注意力页面大小导致高达7.3倍的容量浪费，要么使用静态双池无法适应提示分布的变化，导致内存利用率低下。

Method: 提出非对称虚拟内存分页（AVMP），核心设计包括：(1) 将两种缓存类型分配到物理上分离的池中，但通过统一虚拟地址空间呈现；(2) 当某个池耗尽时，自动将容量从另一个池迁移过来；(3) 迁移仅在分配失败时触发，保证行为确定性。实现为纯Python。

Result: 在RTX 3060 12GB上对270个合成单元和60个ShareGPT轨迹回放单元进行评估。OOM事件下降7.6%，请求吞吐量在合成负载上提升1.83倍到13.3倍，在ShareGPT上提升2.36倍。所有结果通过配对bootstrap 95%置信区间验证。阶段时间分解揭示了两种机制：容量压力工作负载上缩短OOM恢复时间，KV重负载工作负载上加快分配调用。

Conclusion: AVMP有效解决了混合语言模型中两种缓存类型的物理内存管理不匹配问题，显著提升了内存利用率和推理吞吐量。目前为纯Python实现，Triton集成是未来工作方向。

Abstract: Hybrid language models like Jamba mix attention layers with State Space Models (SSMs), creating two memory cache types with opposite profiles: Key-Value (KV) caches grow linearly with sequence length, while SSM states stay fixed per layer. Current inference engines handle this poorly. Unified pools pad SSM states to attention page sizes, wasting up to 7.3x capacity. Static dual pools cannot adapt when prompt distributions shift between requests. We present Asymmetric Virtual Memory Paging (AVMP). The allocator separates the two cache types into physically distinct pools behind a unified virtual address space, and migrates capacity between pools when one runs out. Migration triggers only on allocation failure, keeping behavior deterministic. We evaluate AVMP across 270 synthetic cells plus 60 cells of ShareGPT trace replay on an RTX 3060 12GB. Out-of-Memory events drop 7.6% and request throughput improves 1.83x to 13.3x across synthetic workloads and 2.36x on ShareGPT. All gains hold under paired-bootstrap 95% confidence intervals. A phase-time breakdown reveals two distinct mechanisms: shorter OOM recovery on capacity-pressured workloads, and faster allocation calls on KV-heavy workloads. Implementation is pure Python; Triton integration is future work.

</details>


### [141] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明匹配全后验协方差可以将DDPM的路径KL散度从不可避免的Ω(1/T)降至O(1/T²)，并提出了无需训练和矩阵存储的Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS）来实现这一改进，仅需少量Jacobian-向量积即可取得优于对角协方差基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准各向同性逆协方差在路径KL散度上存在不可避免的Ω(1/T)误差上界，这限制了DDPM在分类器引导等涉及整个逆向轨迹的场景中的性能。作者希望突破这一误差屏障，实现更优的路径空间性能。

Method: 理论分析证明匹配全后验协方差可将路径KL从Ω(1/T)降至O(1/T²)；为实用化提出Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），利用后验均值的Jacobian-向量积实现协方差-向量积，以无矩阵方式近似最优逆协方差，无需存储稠密协方差或训练辅助模型。

Result: LGS的近似误差随Lanczos步数指数衰减，每步仅需一次Jacobian-向量积；仅用三步LGS即可在标准图像基准上超越包括OCM-DDPM在内的强对角协方差基线。

Conclusion: 全协方差匹配在理论上具有显著优势，且通过LGS方法在实践中也成为可行且高效的选择，为快速DDPM采样提供了新方向。

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [142] [AMUSE: Anytime Muon with Stable Gradient Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22432)
*Jueun Kim,Baekrok Shin,Jihun Yun,Beomhan Baek,Minhak Song,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMUSE方法，结合Muon的快速批量进度与Schedule-Free的稳定平均效应，通过时变插值系数实现无学习率调度和任意时间训练，在视觉任务和LLM预训练中持续改进性能-迭代帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在优化中表现优异但其底层机制尚未完全理解，且存在正交化放大主导方向噪声导致振荡轨迹的问题。

Method: 通过河谷损失景观分析Muon机制，提出AMUSE：使用时变插值系数，初始接近快速Muon序列以快速适应，随后逐渐转向稳定平均序列以抑制谷壁振荡。

Result: AMUSE无需学习率调度，支持任意时间训练，在视觉任务和LLM预训练中持续改进性能-迭代帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: AMUSE成功整合了Muon的快速批量进度和Schedule-Free的稳定性，在多种任务上优于(Schedule-Free) AdamW和Muon。
