### [179] [General Revenue Adequacy Conditions for Energy Transport Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21620)
*Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Anatoly Zlotnik,Aleksandr M. Rudkevich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性物理网络流（电力交流/直流潮流、天然气稳态管网）建立了通用的形式化数学框架，分析了收入充足性（revenue adequacy）条件，并验证了该条件在多种能源网络中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场中基于凸性假设的优化调度和定价机制能保证收入充足性（即市场管理者收取的阻塞租金足以结算金融输电权），但交流潮流方程本质非凸，该保证难以推广。同时，天然气市场的区位定价也需要类似的收入充足性及压缩机成本覆盖保证。因此需要建立更一般的理论框架。

Method: 建立非线性物理网络流的一般形式化数学框架，从理论层面推演收入充足性所需的条件，并在直流潮流、交流潮流以及稳态天然气管道网络三种具体场景中验证该条件的成立性。

Result: 所提出的收入充足性条件在DC潮流、AC潮流以及稳态天然气管道网络流中均得到验证，表明该一般化框架能够统一刻画多种非线性物理网络下的市场收入保证问题。

Conclusion: 非线性网络流的收入充足性条件可在一般数学框架下统一表述，并适用于电力（含交直流）和天然气管网，为天然气区位定价机制的设计提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Optimization is widely used to determine the physical and financial exchange of wholesale electricity in organized markets. Guarantees of solution optimality and feasibility rest largely on convexity, which is not in general a characteristic of the governing equations for power grid and gas pipeline networks. Policy decisions that base the scheduling and locational pricing of electricity transactions on optimization rely on the guarantee of revenue adequacy, which ensures that the market administrator will collect enough payments in congestion rents to settle financial transmission rights. Developing a similar mechanism for locational trade valuation of natural gas also requires assurance that pricing outcomes are revenue adequate, and also cover the costs of gas compressor operation. However, it has been shown that the AC power flow equations are in general non-convex and hence conditions for guaranteeing revenue adequacy in optimal power flow solutions are challenging to generalize. In this study, we develop a general formal mathematical setting for nonlinear physical network flows and examine the conditions for revenue adequacy. The result is verified for DC and AC power flow as well as steady-state gas flow in a pipeline network.

</details>


### [180] [On Moment-Based Recovery of Measures with Atomic and Continuous Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21644)
*Ruben Karapetyan,Shenyuan Ma,Aleš Wodecki,Jakub Mareček*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的矩恢复问题，该问题假设测度具有紧支撑并满足温和的分离准则，不仅覆盖有限原子测度，还包含连续分量测度。通过研究GNS构造的谱表示与正交多项式的联系，证明了三种不同情境下的恢复保证，并提出了新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的矩恢复依赖于flat-extension性质，但该性质仅适用于有限原子测度，对于许多测度类（含连续分量的测度）并不成立。因此需要一种更通用的恢复问题框架，能同时处理原子测度和连续分量测度。

Method: 通过研究Gelfand-Naimark-Segal (GNS)构造的谱表示及其与正交多项式的联系，在假设测度具有紧支撑且满足温和分离准则的条件下，分析了三种不同情境下的恢复保证。

Result: 证明了三种不同情境下的恢复保证，为广泛用于原子测度矩恢复的算法提供了新的理论洞见，并据此提出了新算法，通过基准测试验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架扩大了矩恢复的适用范围，弥合了有限原子测度与含连续分量测度之间的理论鸿沟，并为实际算法设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recovering probability measures from moments is a central theme in statistics and optimization. In particular, we focus on the recovery of measures from moments and pseudo-moments, which may come from solving the moment-SOS hierarchy in one dimension. A typical strategy when recovering a measure from moments is to verify the flat-extension property, which certifies that the underlying measure is finitely atomic and ultimately leads to recovery. For many classes of measures, however, the flat extension never occurs and thus if one aims to recover the measure corresponding to the moments, assumptions need to be made. We formulate a new kind of recovery problem, where one assumes that the measure has compact support and a fulfills a mild separation criterion. The key feature of this recovery problem formulation is that it covers not only finitely atomic measures, but also measures with continuous components. We study this new problem and describe three situations in which different guarantees can be proven. These guarantees are developed by studying the spectral representation of the Gelfand-Naimark-Segal construction and its connection to orthogonal polynomials, which ultimately allows us to provide several additional insights, which apply to algorithms widely used for the recovery of atomic measures from moments. Furthermore, the statements proven lead to novel algorithms, which we benchmark, further confirming the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [181] [Spectral analysis of the logit mapping and implications for stochastic user equilibrium algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21843)
*Debojjal Bagchi,Stephen D. Boyles*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过分析随机用户均衡（SUE）中Logit映射的雅可比矩阵结构，提出两种改进的路径算法：自适应常数步长MSA（线性收敛）和基于牛顿法的超线性收敛算法，首次在大规模网络（芝加哥区域、费城）上报告了运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径SUE算法收敛速度慢或计算代价高（如Hessian稠密矩阵），缺乏在大规模网络上的高效求解方法。

Method: (1) 解析Logit映射雅可比矩阵结构，证明其分解为一个零化可行路径流差矩阵和一个特征值非正实数的矩阵（在单调非递减可分链路成本下）；(2) 基于此提出自适应常数步长MSA，保持全局收敛同时实现渐近线性收敛；(3) 将SUE重写为求根问题，设计利用雅可比矩阵结构的牛顿法，避免稠密Hessian和流形优化。

Result: 小常数步长MSA以速率1-s线性收敛；自适应步长MSA保持全局收敛且渐近线性收敛；牛顿法在多数测试网络上呈超线性收敛；在大规模网络或高需求场景下优于现有方法；首次在芝加哥区域和费城网络上报告Logit SUE运行时间。

Conclusion: Logit映射雅可比矩阵的结构特性可被有效利用来设计高效SUE算法，为大规模网络上的算法开发提供了基准。

Abstract: We analyze the Jacobian of the logit mapping for stochastic user equilibrium (SUE) and use it to develop two improved algorithms for path-based SUE. We show that the Jacobian decomposes into two matrices: one that annihilates differences of feasible path flow vectors, and another whose eigenvalues are all non-positive reals, provided link costs are monotone non-decreasing and separable. Using these properties, we first show that the method of successive averages (MSA) with a small constant step-size $s$ converges linearly at a rate $1-s$, with the largest admissible step-size depending on the eigenvalues of the Jacobian of the logit mapping. Building on this result, we develop an adaptive constant step-size rule that retains the global convergence of MSA while achieving asymptotic linear convergence. Our second algorithm is a Newton-based method using a reformulation of SUE as a root-finding problem. Unlike gradient-projection approaches that operate on the Hessian of the SUE objective function (a dense matrix), our method exploits the structure of the Jacobian of the logit mapping, making computations tractable and removing the need for manifold optimization. Numerical experiments show superlinear convergence on most tested networks, with our methods outperforming existing approaches on large networks or when demand is high. To our knowledge, this article is the first to report runtimes for logit-based SUE on networks as large as Chicago Regional and Philadelphia, providing a benchmark for future algorithmic development.

</details>


### [182] [Proximal Gradient-based Low Rank Tensor Decomposition for State Dependent Riccati Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21885)
*Jiahua Jiang,Carmeliza Navasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模离散维控制系统的偏微分方程最优控制问题，提出使用CP张量分解结合稀疏优化与灵活混合方法进行降阶建模，进而高效求解状态依赖Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模离散维控制系统带来的维数灾难问题，使得最优控制问题在计算上可行。

Method: 从快照数据构建张量数据集，通过CP（Canonical Polyadic）分解提取低秩张量基元；采用稀疏优化和灵活混合方法实现降维，获得低秩CP张量基；在降阶模型上构建状态依赖Riccati方程并高效求解。

Result: 得到降阶的最优控制问题，其状态依赖Riccati方程可高效求解。

Conclusion: CP张量分解结合稀疏优化与混合方法能有效对大规模离散控制系统的PDE最优控制问题进行降阶，提升计算效率。

Abstract: We address the optimal control problems arising from partial differential equations with large discrete dimensional control systems. To obtain reduced order models, we find basis elements from the canonical polyadic (CP) decomposition. Tensor datasets are from snapshots of the large models. Our method to reduce the control system is to use dimensionality reduction approaches through sparse optimization and flexible hybrid methods is to obtain low rank CP tensor basis elements. The reduced optimal control problem leads to reduced state-dependent Riccati Equations which can be solved efficiently.

</details>


### [183] [Exact Uniform L1 Spacing for Solow-Polasky Diversity on Lines and Ordered Pareto Fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21922)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich,Mahboubeh Nezhadmoghaddam,Jesús Guillermo Falcón Cardona*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维及有序度量集上逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性（即指数核的有限度量幅度）的固定基数最大化问题。证明了区间[0,1]上每个k≥2的唯一最大化k点子集是等间距集，该结论通过等距映射推广到有序ℓ₁曲线上，并应用于单调双目标Pareto前沿的均匀间距最优有限逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性（即指数核下的有限度量幅度）在固定基数子集选择中的最大化问题，旨在理解何种点集配置能使该多样性指标达到最优，并为多目标优化中Pareto前沿的均匀采样提供理论依据。

Method: 从已知的有限线间隙公式出发，将超量逆矩阵多样性表示为相邻间隙函数的和；通过区间定理证明等间距集是唯一最优解；利用归一化非增距离核的逆问题推导出指数族是该加性结构的必然结果；通过等距映射将结论推广到有序ℓ₁曲线。

Result: (1) 对任意k≥2，[0,1]上最大化Solow-Polasky多样性的唯一k点子集是等间距集；(2) 在归一化非增距离核中，具有相邻间隙加性结构必然是指数核；(3) 结论通过等距映射推广到有序ℓ₁曲线，最优子集在累积ℓ₁长度上均匀分布；(4) 单调双目标Pareto前沿的最优有限逼近在累积目标空间变化上均匀分布。

Conclusion: 均匀间距是逆矩阵Solow-Polasky多样性在实数线上固定基数最大化时的自然选择，该性质通过ℓ₁等距映射可推广至曲线及Pareto前沿逼近问题，为多目标优化中的代表性子集选择提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study fixed-cardinality maximization of the inverse-matrix Solow--Polasky diversity, equivalently finite metric magnitude for the exponential kernel, on one-dimensional and ordered metric sets. The analysis starts from the known finite-line gap formula for the exponential kernel, which writes the excess inverse-matrix diversity as a sum of functions of consecutive gaps. Building on this formula, the main interval theorem proves that, for every $k\geq 2$, the unique maximizing $k$-point subset of $[0,1]$ is the equally spaced set. Thus the objective selects a uniform gap representation on the real line. A converse kernel proposition shows that, among normalized non-increasing distance kernels, requiring the corresponding adjacent-gap additive structure forces the exponential family. Further results transfer the interval theorem to ordered $\ell_1$ (L1, or Manhattan) curves by isometry: the maximizing sets are uniform in accumulated $\ell_1$ length. As a consequence, monotone biobjective Pareto fronts admit Solow--Polasky optimal finite approximations that are uniformly spaced in accumulated objective-space change, a natural representation when all parts of a continuous front should be covered. Examples, including a dense connected front and a finite disconnected ZDT3 front, illustrate how the continuous uniform-gap result appears on discrete candidate sets.
  Solow-Polasky diversity; diversity measures; finite metric magnitude; L1 distance; uniform spacing; Pareto-front approximation; multiobjective optimization; fixed-cardinality subset selection

</details>


### [184] [Achieving Directional-Stationarity from a Single Random Direction Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22045)
*Dan Greenstein,Nadav Hallak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对约束非光滑非凸优化问题，提出通过随机方向探索步骤来保证方向平稳性（d-stationarity），该步骤可增强任何基础优化方法，且几乎必然确保所有聚点都是d-平稳的，同时保持基础方法的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束非光滑非凸优化中，标准方法通常只能保证弱平稳性，而实现方向平稳性（d-stationarity）仍然困难。本文旨在利用随机方向探索步骤，在温和的正则性条件（局部Lipschitz连续性和方向导数存在且连续）下获得强最优性保证。

Method: 提出一种随机方向探索步骤，该步骤采样一个方向和步长，基于函数值比较接受候选点。该步骤可增强任何基础优化方法，只需单次探索步骤即可实现d-stationarity。

Result: 该方法保证所有聚点几乎必然都是d-平稳的，且与基础方法的行为无关。同时，该方法能保持基础方法的收敛速率，例如在DCA和prox-linear型方案中已得到验证。数值实验验证了探索步骤的效果和保证。

Conclusion: 随机方向探索步骤是一种简单有效的方式，可以增强现有优化方法，使其在温和正则条件下获得更强的d-平稳性保证，且不影响收敛速率。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of obtaining strong optimality guarantees in constrained nonsmooth nonconvex optimization under mild regularity conditions, namely local Lipschitz continuity and existence and continuity of directional derivatives. While standard methods typically ensure weak stationarity notions, achieving directional (d-)stationarity remains nontrivial. We show that a random direction exploration step is sufficient to attain d-stationarity. The proposed approach augments any base optimization method with a single exploration step that samples a direction and step size and accepts the candidate based on a function value comparison. The resulting scheme guarantees that all accumulation points are d-stationary almost surely, independently of the behavior of the underlying method. Moreover, it preserves convergence rates of the base method, as established for DCA and prox-linear-type schemes. The theoretical results are complemented by numerical experiments illustrating the effect and guarantees of the exploration step.

</details>


### [185] [Online Optimization with Unknown Time-Varying Parameters from Noisy Gradient Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22251)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合控制理论和系统辨识的在线优化算法，用于处理成本函数中隐藏参数未知且动态变化的问题，通过Gauss-Markov估计重构参数、工具变量法辨识动态、预测未来最小化器，并给出了期望跟踪误差的理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线优化方法通常假设成本函数完全可观测或隐藏参数具有已知动态，但实际中成本函数依赖于未知动态演变的不可测潜变量，仅能获得有限噪声梯度测量，亟需一种能同时处理参数重构、动态辨识与预测的统计算法。

Method: (1) 使用Gauss-Markov估计器从含噪梯度观测中重构潜藏参数；(2) 利用工具变量估计器辨识未知的线性随机动态；(3) 基于辨识结果预测未来参数并计算最优决策。算法融合了控制理论中的状态估计与系统辨识方法。

Result: 推导了期望跟踪误差的理论上界，并通过数值算例验证了算法在不同设定下的有效性，表明该方法在参数动态未知且仅有无噪梯度观测下仍能实现良好的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功将控制理论的估计与辨识工具应用于在线优化，为处理隐藏参数未知动力学问题提供了一种可理论保证的实用解决方案，拓宽了在线优化的应用场景。

Abstract: We study online optimization problems in which the cost function depends on latent, time-varying parameters that are unmeasurable and governed by unknown dynamics. Specifically, we consider a strongly convex cost function whose linear term evolves according to unknown linear stochastic dynamics, while the algorithm has access only to finite noisy gradient measurements. We propose a solution that uses control theoretic tools to reconstruct the latent parameters from gradient observations using a Gauss-Markov estimator, then identifies the parameter dynamics using an instrumental-variable estimator, and finally forecasts the parameters to compute the future minimizer. We provide a bound on the expected tracking error. We illustrate the effectiveness of our algorithm on a series of numerical examples.

</details>


### [186] [Incentive-Aligned Vehicle-to-Vehicle Energy Trading via Nash-Integrated Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22363)
*Yujin Lin,Yue Yang,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Nash-MADDPG方法，将纳什讨价还价解融入多智能体深度确定性策略梯度，用于V2V能量交易，相比双拍卖方法在社会福利、交易量和公平性上分别提升61.6%、62.9%和40.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2V能量交易方法要么依赖集中式优化导致计算局限，要么缺乏公平性保障，难以协调具有不同充电需求和不确定到达-离开时间的自利电动汽车代理。

Method: 将纳什讨价还价解（Nash Bargaining Solution）集成到多智能体深度确定性策略梯度（MADDPG）中。纳什讨价还价确定高效的双边定价，纳什引导的价格邻近奖励引导代理学习向讨价还价最优策略收敛。

Result: 在30天连续运行中，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，公平性（Jain指数）提升40.1%。6-100个代理规模测试验证了可扩展性，定价稳定接近纳什讨价还价基准。

Conclusion: Nash-MADDPG方法能有效协调自利电动汽车代理进行去中心化V2V能量交易，兼具效率、公平性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) energy trading enables decentralized peer-to-peer energy exchange among electric vehicles (EVs), reducing grid dependency while monetizing surplus capacity. However, coordinating self-interested EV agents with diverse charging needs and uncertain arrival-departure schedules remains challenging. Existing approaches either require centralized optimization with computational limitations or lack fairness guarantees. This paper integrates Nash Bargaining Solution into Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient, namely Nash-MADDPG, for incentive-aligned V2V energy trading. Nash bargaining determines efficient bilateral pricing, while Nash-guided price proximity rewards align agent learning toward bargaining-optimal strategies. Evaluation over 30-day continuous operation demonstrates an improvement of 61.6% in social welfare and 62.9% improvement in trading volume over Double Auction, while achieving superior fairness, such as 40.1% improvement in Jain's index. Testing across 6-100 agents over a 30-day horizon with continuous vehicle turnover confirms scalability across population size and empirically stable pricing near the Nash Bargaining benchmark.

</details>


### [187] [Global Convergence of Control-Based Lagrangian Flows for Non-Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22486)
*Simone Pirrera,Francesco Ripa,Daniele Astolfi,Sophie M. Fosson,Vito Cerone,Diego Regruto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于控制理论拉格朗日方法的等式约束优化连续时间动力学，证明了满足约束流形上凸性条件的非凸问题的全局指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收敛结果依赖于目标函数的强凸性或有界性假设，本文旨在利用约束的几何结构来放宽这些条件。

Method: 采用比例积分和反馈线性化控制器诱导的动力学，利用约束流形的几何结构进行分析。

Result: 对于在约束流形上满足适当凸性性质的非凸问题，证明了全局指数收敛。

Conclusion: 通过几何结构分析，在更宽松的条件下（非凸但流形凸）实现了全局指数收敛，优于传统方法。

Abstract: This paper studies the flows of continuous-time dynamics for equality-constrained optimization based on control-theoretic Lagrangian methods. In particular, we consider dynamics induced by proportional-integral and feedback linearization controllers, which have been recently proposed as alternatives to primal-dual gradient methods. Unlike existing convergence results, which rely on strong convexity of the objective function or boundedness assumptions, we exploit the geometric structure induced by the constraints. Specifically, we show global exponential convergence for non-convex problems that satisfy a suitable convexity property when restricted to the constraint manifold.

</details>


### [188] [A conditional-gradient-based single-loop augmented Lagrangian method for inequality constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22539)
*Xiaozhou Wang,Ting Kei Pong,Zev Woodstock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题，提出了一种将增广拉格朗日方法与条件梯度法相结合的算法，在两种步长规则下取得了与已知最优复杂度匹配的收敛率，并在均匀凸集指示函数情形下获得了加速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增广拉格朗日方法在处理带有多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题时，子问题求解往往需要投影操作，计算成本高。本文希望利用条件梯度法（无需投影，仅需线性最小化预言）来替代子问题求解，从而拓展AL方法的适用范围并保持高效性。

Method: 算法每次迭代包含两步：(1) 对增广拉格朗日函数执行一步条件梯度法（使用开环步长或短步长）；(2) 采用递减的对偶步长更新对偶变量。该方法继承了经典AL方法的框架，但用CG步替代了精确子问题求解。

Result: 在两种标准步长规则下，算法取得了与已知最优复杂度匹配的收敛率。当h为均匀凸集的指示函数时，进一步建立了加速收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的AL-CG混合算法在保持条件梯度法无投影优点的同时，达到了该类问题的最优复杂度，为处理大规模带约束复合凸优化问题提供了一种高效且理论上可证明的求解方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the sum of a Lipschitz differentiable convex function $f$ and a proper closed convex function $h$ that admits efficient linear minimization oracles, subject to multiple smooth convex inequality constraints. We adapt the classical augmented Lagrangian (AL) method for these problems: in each iteration, our algorithm consists of one step of the conditional gradient (CG) method applied to the AL function, followed by an update of the dual variable as in classical AL methods with a diminishing dual stepsize. We study the convergence rate of our algorithm under two standard stepsize rules for the CG method, namely, an open-loop stepsize and the short stepsize, and obtain a convergence rate that matches the best-known complexity for this class of problems. We also establish accelerated rates when $h$ is the indicator function of a uniformly convex set.

</details>


### [189] [Output regulation via input-output data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22562)
*Andrea Bisoffi,Wenjie Liu,Zhongjie Hu,Claudio De Persis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对MIMO离散时间线性系统，提出了一种纯数据驱动的输出调节方法，无需系统模型知识，仅利用受未知外生信号噪声影响的输入输出数据设计反馈控制器，以渐近消除外生信号对输出的影响。控制器设计通过半定规划在辅助系统上完成，并通过严格分析保证设计结果适用于原系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不知道系统模型的情况下，仅从受噪声污染的输入输出数据出发，解决MIMO线性系统的输出调节问题（即渐近抑制未知外生信号对输出的影响）。

Method: 将输出调节问题转化为半定规划（SDP）问题，在构造的辅助系统上进行控制器设计，并通过严格的理论分析建立辅助系统与原系统之间的关系，保证设计结果可迁移至原系统。

Result: 提出了一种纯数据驱动的MIMO线性系统输出调节控制器设计方法，能够有效渐近消除未知外生信号对输出的影响。

Conclusion: 本文证明了无需系统模型知识，仅利用受噪声影响的输入输出数据即可解决MIMO线性系统的输出调节问题，且控制器可通过半定规划高效求解。

Abstract: From a multi-input-multi-output (MIMO) discrete-time linear system, we collect input-output data affected by noise in the form of an unknown exosignal and, from these data points (without knowledge of the system model), we design a feedback controller that asymptotically annihilates the effect of that exosignal on the output. This amounts to solving an output regulation problem purely from input-output data, for MIMO linear systems. The design of the controller corresponds to a semidefinite program and is pursued on a suitable auxiliary system. Such design carries over from the auxiliary system to the original one by a rigorous examination of the relation between the solutions of the two systems.

</details>


### [190] [Characterization of Normalizer of Lie Superalgebra and its Application to Control Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22694)
*Aroonima Sahoo,Kishor Chandra Pati,Tofan Kumar Khuntia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究李超群上同时包含玻色子和费米子变量的控制系统，利用左不变向量场的李超子代数的正规化子刻画控制系统，并详细研究了线性控制系统及其可控性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称理论中同时包含玻色子和费米子变量的动力系统具有重要作用，然而这类系统在控制理论视角下的研究尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以李超群为配置空间，利用左不变向量场的李超子代数的正规化子来描述控制系统，并对线性控制系统进行详细分析，提出可控性判据。

Result: 提出了李超群上线性控制系统的可控性判据，并给出了相应的实例说明。

Conclusion: 本文为超对称动力系统的控制问题提供了理论框架，所提出的可控性判据为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The dynamical systems having both bosonic and fermionic variables play an important role in the theory of supersymmetry. This article addresses the control problems including both bosonic and fermionic variables on Lie supergroup as the configuration space. Here, the control systems are characterized using the normalizer of Lie subsuperalgebra of left-invariant vector fields in the Lie superalgebra of all smooth vector fields of Lie supergroup. Then, the linear control system is studied in detail and its controllability criterion is proposed along with suitable examples.

</details>


### [191] [Optimization over the intersection of manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22736)
*Yan Yang,Bin Gao,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了流形交集中"干净相交"与"本质横截性"的等价性，并提出一种仅在一个流形上进行retraction、沿两个正交方向更新的几何优化方法，在稀疏与低秩优化等应用中展示了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个流形交集上的优化问题广泛存在，但可行区域的耦合几何结构使优化困难，需要一种可操作且高效的几何方法。

Method: 证明干净相交与本质横截性等价，从而获得到交集切空间的可计算投影；提出仅在一个流形上使用retraction，沿两个正交方向（一个负责趋近另一个流形，一个负责降低目标函数）更新迭代点。

Result: 在本质横截性条件下推导了可行性与最优性测度的收敛率，证明每个聚点都是一阶稳定点。在球面数据拟合、双曲嵌入逼近和压缩模式计算等任务中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了双流形交集优化问题，理论保障充分，数值表现良好。

Abstract: Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MOOD的基准测试，用于系统评估LLM监控管线能否检测到分布外（OOD）的对齐失败。研究发现守卫模型（安全分类器）在OOD场景下泛化能力不足，而将守卫模型与基于马氏距离和困惑度的OOD检测器结合，可将召回率从39%提升至45%，且OOD检测带来的召回率增益超过将守卫模型参数扩大20倍的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全和对齐失败常发生在分布外（OOD）场景中，即模型开发者未曾预料到的异常提示或回复模式。然而，现有的监控管线能否有效检测这些OOD对齐失败尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 构建MOOD基准测试，包含受限训练集和七个分布外测试集；训练自定义监控器；测试守卫模型与四种OOD检测器（马氏距离、困惑度等）的组合方案。

Result: 守卫模型在OOD场景下泛化失败；将守卫模型与马氏距离和困惑度OOD检测器结合，召回率从39%提升至45%；OOD检测带来的召回率增益超过将守卫模型参数量扩大20倍的效果。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组成部分，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [193] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TO-Agents是一个多智能体AI框架，将自然语言设计意图与迭代拓扑优化相连接，通过视觉-语言推理和独立评判智能体自动调整求解器参数，在60%的试验中生成符合审美偏好的设计，相比消融基线成功率提升6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化中，设计者需要手动将定性设计意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）转化为与这些偏好无直接关联的求解器设置，缺乏自然语言到优化参数的直接映射。

Method: 提出TO-Agents多智能体框架：将自然语言问题描述转换为验证后的求解器输入，运行拓扑优化求解器，渲染3D拓扑结果，使用多视图视觉-语言推理和独立评判智能体对结果进行评判并修订求解器参数。包含制造智能体进行后处理以实现端到端设计。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准测试和手机支架产品设计两个任务中，针对自然树形层次分支结构的美学偏好，系统在10次独立重复试验中执行4次修订循环。TO-Agents在60%的试验中生成至少一个符合偏好的设计，成功率比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管线高出6倍。评判智能体评分和人工评估表明管线能识别有效参数杠杆、从错误修订中恢复并扩展设计探索。

Conclusion: 智能体拓扑优化可以将设计师从底层参数调优转向更高层次的形式与功能规范，同时需要为可靠的自主工程设计建立安全保障机制。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [194] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对USC Shoah基金会和耶鲁Fortunoff视频档案馆超过1600份证词的大规模计算分析，本研究证实了两种口述证词风格的结构性差异，但也揭示了大量重叠，挑战了简单的"结构化vs自由形式"二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究领域长期存在一个被广泛接受但缺乏大规模实证检验的论断：USC Shoah基金会的访谈遵循结构化、访谈者引导的形式，而耶鲁Fortunoff视频档案馆则偏好自由、开放式的风格。这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案馆建设，但其有效性尚未得到系统性的量化验证。

Method: 对超过1600份来自两个档案馆的证词进行大规模计算分析，采用话语分割、主题建模和基于大语言模型的分析方法，通过主题连贯性、访谈者-幸存者互动动态和问题类型分布来量化证词的"结构化"程度。

Result: 研究结果总体上证实了先前研究识别的结构性差异，但同时也揭示了两个档案集之间存在显著重叠——无论是单个访谈内部还是跨越常见的叙事模式，这表明简单的"结构化vs自由形式"二分法过于简化。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅重新审视了大屠杀研究中的一个基础性论断，还提供了一种可扩展、可复制的比较语料库分析框架，为数字口述历史、叙事分析和公民科学注释平台设计提供了更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [195] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindLoom是一个通过组合式思维模式工程合成前沿推理数据的框架。它先将困难问题的已验证解分解为思维模式链，再训练检索模型匹配问题状态与思维模式，迭代组合生成新问题，并通过难度标注和答案验证生成微调数据。在九个覆盖五个STEM学科和四个数学推理任务的基准测试上，微调模型取得了优于基线模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法对问题难度的结构性因素可见性有限，导致数据多样性狭窄且难度控制不稳定，难以系统地产生前沿水平的推理数据。

Method: MindLoom框架包含四个步骤：(1) 将困难问题的已验证解分解为思维模式链（thought mode chains）；(2) 训练检索模型匹配问题状态与兼容的思维模式；(3) 迭代应用检索到的思维模式到种子问题，结合分布对齐采样组合新问题；(4) 基于rollout的评判阶段标注难度并生成经评判为正确的答案用于监督微调。

Result: 在九个覆盖五个STEM学科和四个数学推理任务的基准测试上，经MindLoom生成数据微调的模型在性能上优于基础模型、蒸馏方法和外部数据基线。消融实验验证了各组件的贡献，进一步分析表明MindLoom覆盖了广泛的推理模式并具有良好的难度控制能力。

Conclusion: 将问题难度视为原子知识-推理转换（思维模式）的累积，并通过组合式思维模式工程合成数据，能够有效生成多样且难度可控的高质量推理数据，提升模型推理能力。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [196] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，一个证据模型原型，通过数据模型扩展来改进AOP-Wiki的FAIR性、AI就绪性及与NAM的集成，以支持计算生成的AOP和定量AOP，从而服务于监管科学及生物医学和One Health领域的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AOP-Wiki作为全球AOP存储库，其现有数据模型和应用基础设施限制了AOP的持续增长。同时，agentic AI的变革性力量为AOP-Wiki数据现代化提供了新机遇，需要利用AOP核心原则来指导AI对AOP相关信息的聚合与结构化。

Method: 通过开发AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0（第三个证据模型原型），重点关注三方面解决方案：AOP-Wiki内部质量改进、增强AOP FAIR性和AI就绪性的证据结构化、以及AOP框架与NAMs之间的集成改进。

Result: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0具体展示了数据模型扩展，以及如何将AOP-Wiki转变以更好地服务监管科学、生物医学和One Health等新兴应用场景。

Conclusion: 该工作为支持计算生成的AOP和定量AOP（qAOP）奠定基础，以服务于下一代风险评估。AOP-Wiki的现代化将借助agentic AI的推动力，实现从传统监管科学向更广泛生物医学和One Health领域应用的扩展。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [197] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过让AI系统对明显不属于某类别的物体（如“橄榄是交通工具吗？”）进行归属判断，该研究揭示了AI与人类在概念边界理解上的系统性差异，并展示了这些差异如何转化为有安全隐患的下游行为问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过询问典型类别成员（如“汽车是交通工具吗？”）来探测AI的概念理解，但这容易让模型依赖训练数据中的表面模式。作者希望采用另一种策略，通过询问不合理类别成员来更真实地刻画AI概念边界的认知质量。

Method: 基于Rosch和Mervis的经典心理学研究，设计了一套包含正常类别归属和错配类别归属的完整任务集合。将AI系统的物体到上位类别的分配结果与人类参与者的判断进行比较。

Result: AI模型在多个概念上与人类存在显著差异：将“词语”错误归入“交通工具”和“衣服”类别；将多个“蔬菜”类别成员识别为“水果”；将非武器类别的样例分配给“武器”类别。

Conclusion: AI系统对日常概念的边界理解与人类存在系统性偏差，这些概念对齐问题会转化为有安全影响的下游行为，对开发安全可靠的AI系统构成挑战。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [198] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过逻辑推理任务实验发现，AI使用频率与技能发展呈负相关，且这一关系受AI信息含量的调节：低信息AI损害长期学习，高信息AI能提升短期表现但效果存在异质性，说明AI既可能补充也可能替代人类技能发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正越来越多地融入人类问题解决过程，但其对个体技能发展的影响尚不明确，本研究旨在探究AI使用及AI信息含量如何塑造学习效果。

Method: 在受控的逻辑推理任务中，参与者可按需获取AI辅助，通过比较不同AI使用频率和信息含量的组别来评估技能发展效果。

Result: 重度AI使用者技能发展较弱，表现低于可比同伴；轻度AI使用者表现与未使用AI的匹配用户相似。低信息AI既未提升即时表现，移除AI后表现也未维持，与整体学习效果较差相关；高信息AI提升了短期表现且平均未降低移除AI后的结果，但存在异质性效应。

Conclusion: AI在不同情境下可以互补人类技能发展（通过增强独立推理），也可能成为替代品（削弱独立推理）。因此，合理监管AI的访问和使用对促进技能发展至关重要。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [199] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将拒绝抑制重新理解为潜在空间逃逸攻击，通过将表示投影到决策边界之外来突破安全对齐模型的安全限制，在多种模型上实现了最优攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过消融拒绝方向来抑制安全对齐模型拒绝行为的方法虽然有效，但缺乏对潜在空间变换的理论解释，且其效果止步于决策边界，无法充分压制拒绝行为。

Method: 将拒绝抑制建模为针对线性探针的潜在空间逃逸攻击，将先前工作的均值差方向视为线性探针，消融操作等价于投影到决策边界的最小置信度逃逸攻击；进一步提出受控潜在空间逃逸攻击（CLE），将表示投影到决策边界之外并优化置信度。

Result: 在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上取得了最先进的攻击成功率，超越了现有的拒绝消融基线和专用越狱攻击方法。

Conclusion: 从逃逸攻击视角为现有拒绝抑制方法提供了理论解释，并揭示了其局限性，通过跨越决策边界实现了更强的攻击效果。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [200] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AttuneBench基准，基于200个真实多轮人-模型对话，评估LLM在情绪智能方面的四项独立能力：情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断。发现这些能力彼此独立，偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情绪标签准确度更具模型区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情绪智能基准依赖于合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法直接测量模型在真实多轮对话中推断和响应参与者情绪状态的能力。

Method: 收集200个真实多轮人-模型对话，参与者与匿名LLM交互并逐轮标注自己的情绪状态、模型行为以及偏好的响应。评估11个模型在情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断四个维度的表现。

Result: 模型在四项能力上的排名基本独立，表明情绪智能行为可分解为可分离的能力。偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情绪标签准确度更具模型区分度。

Conclusion: 情绪智能行为需要预测特定用户在上下文中期望的响应类型，这是聚合评分和单轮/合成格式无法直接捕捉的。AttuneBench为评估这些能力并诊断模型在情绪对话中的优缺点提供了框架。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [201] [SMDD-Bench: Can LLMs Solve Real-World Small Molecule Drug Design Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21740)
*Kevin Han,Renfei Zhang,Kathy Wei,Hamed Mahdavi,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Amir Farimani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMDD-Bench，一个包含502个可解任务实例的多轮、长周期智能体基准，用于标准化评估LLM智能体在小分子药物设计上的能力。基准涵盖5种任务类型和102个蛋白质靶点，测试了7个前沿LLM，表现最好的GPT5.4仅解决了40.2%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在小分子药物设计任务上的评估方法存在临时性强、过于简单、规模有限或仅限于单轮问答等问题，缺乏标准化的评估基准。

Method: 构建了SMDD-Bench基准，包含502个保证可解的任务实例，覆盖5种任务类型（2D药效团识别、相互作用点发现、骨架跃迁、先导化合物优化、片段组装），涉及102个独特蛋白质靶标，并设置有限次数的oracle调用限制以测试规划能力。

Result: 对7个前沿开源和闭源LLM进行基准测试，表现最好的GPT5.4仅解决了40.2%的任务，表明现有LLM智能体在药物设计任务上仍有显著不足。

Conclusion: SMDD-Bench可作为标准化测试平台，推动领域内针对完全自主的计算药物设计方向训练和评估LLM智能体的研究。

Abstract: LLM agents have incredible potential for scientific discovery applications. However, the performance of LLM agents on real-world, small molecule drug design (SMDD) tasks across diverse chemistries and targets is unclear. Current evaluation methods are either ad hoc, too simple for real-world discovery, limited in scale, or restricted to single-turn question answering. In effort to standardize the evaluation of LLM agents on small molecule design, we introduce SMDD-Bench, a challenging, multi-turn, long-horizon agentic benchmark consisting of 502 guaranteed-solvable task instances spanning 5 task types: 2D Pharmacophore Identification, Interaction Point Discovery, Scaffold Hopping, Lead Optimization, and Fragment Assembly. SMDD-Bench tasks span a wide region of chemical space and involve 102 unique protein targets. Completely solving the benchmark would require having strong chemical and biological reasoning and 3D intuition, understanding specialized tool use, and displaying planning expertise over a limited number of oracle calls. We benchmark 7 frontier open and closed source LLMs and find even the most performant LLM, GPT5.4, solves only 40.2\% of tasks. We hope SMDD-Bench provides a standardized testbed to invigorate the field towards training and evaluating LLM agents for fully autonomous computational drug design. We host a public leaderboard at smddbench.com .

</details>


### [202] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在系统性偏差：这些分数实际反映的是平台用户构成而非劳动力市场结构。通过控制其他变量仅改变数据来源，后ChatGPT时期的就业系数差异可达1.9倍；同一供应商内部消费者版与企业版估计结果符号相反。按BLS劳动力份额重新加权后估计值衰减42-93%。作者形式化了非经典测量误差，推导了就业弹性的概率极限和部分识别边界，发现偏差会低估替代效应而非增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛采用AI平台对话日志（如ChatGPT使用数据）来测量职业暴露度，但这种测量方法可能存在系统性偏差——它更多反映的是平台用户的人口统计学特征，而非真实劳动力市场的职业结构。作者旨在量化这种偏差并揭示其对估计就业效应的误导性影响。

Method: 采用因果推断框架，固定结果变量、样本、控制变量和估计方法，仅改变测量暴露度的平台数据来源（不同平台、同一平台的不同版本），比较估计系数的差异。同时使用BLS劳动力份额进行重新加权，并建立非经典测量误差的计量经济学模型，推导概率极限和部分识别边界。

Result: 1) 仅改变平台输入，后ChatGPT就业系数变化1.9倍；2) 同一供应商的消费者版与企业版估计结果符号相反；3) 按BLS劳动力份额加权后估计值衰减42-93%。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在严重的非经典测量误差，导致对AI就业效应的估计产生系统性偏差，且偏差方向为低估替代效应、高估增强效应。研究者需要谨慎处理这些测量问题，否则会得出误导性的政策结论。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [203] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种将因果发现与双极论证框架(BAF)相结合的XAI方法，通过半稳定语义识别支持性和对立性特征交互，从而解释模型为何做出特定预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法能识别哪些特征与模型预测相关，但无法阐明为何做出特定决策，缺乏因果层面的解释能力。

Method: 首先使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系，然后将因果关系转化为双极论证框架(BAF)以表示特征间的支持和反对交互，最后通过半稳定语义(semi-stable semantics)找到能够解释预测结果的特征扩展集。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上进行了实验，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 提出的因果-论证结合方法能够提供比传统事后解释方法更深入的因果层面解释，阐明模型为何做出特定预测。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [204] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Flexible Job Shop Scheduling with Random Job Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22773)
*Yu Tang,Muhammad Zakwan,Efe Balta,John Lygeros,Alisa Rupenyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件的深度强化学习（DRL）方法，使用近端策略优化（PPO）算法和轻量级多层感知机（MLP），通过选择已有的调度规则来求解具有随机作业到达的柔性作业车间调度问题（FJSP），以最小化所有作业的总完工时间。实验表明，该方法在不同异构性和作业到达率的数据集上均优于任何单一调度规则，并且在数据集异构时表现尤其接近到达触发的混合整数线性规划（MILP）解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FJSP面临两个主要挑战：未来作业的不可预测到达以及问题的组合复杂性，传统混合整数线性规划求解器难以处理。

Method: 采用事件驱动的深度强化学习框架，使用PPO算法和轻量级MLP网络训练智能体，状态表示直接从环境中获取，动作空间限定为从一组已有的调度规则中选择。

Result: DRL方法在具有不同异构性和作业到达率的数据集上均优于任何单一调度规则；与到达触发的MILP解法相比，在异构数据集上表现良好。

Conclusion: 基于事件的DRL方法能够有效解决带有随机作业到达的FJSP，尤其是在数据集异构性较高时，性能接近MILP方法。

Abstract: The Flexible Job Shop Scheduling Problem (FJSP) is the optimal allocation of a set of jobs to machines. Two primary challenges persist in FJSP: the unpredictable arrival of future jobs and the combinatorial complexity of the problem, rendering it intractable for conventional mixed-integer linear programming solvers. This paper proposes an event-based \gls{DRL} approach to solve FJSP with random job arrivals. Specifically, we employ the Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm and use lightweight Multi-Layer Perceptrons to train the \gls{DRL} agent for minimizing the total completion time of all jobs. We design the state representation to be directly accessible from the environment, and limit the learning agent to selecting from among a set of well-established dispatching rules. Simulations show that our \gls{DRL} approach outperforms any of the individual dispatching rules on datasets with varying heterogeneity and job arrival rates. We benchmark our \gls{DRL} against an arrival-triggered mixed-integer linear programming solution and show that our method achieves good performance especially when the datasets are heterogeneous.

</details>


### [205] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过综述70篇论文和调查106位专家，建立了AI谄媚行为的分类体系，区分了针对用户立场/信念与个人特质/情感、以及显性直接与隐晦间接的行为类型。研究发现当前研究集中于显性的信念谄媚，而隐性的个人导向行为被忽视；专家虽一致认为谄媚是重大问题（94.3%），但对具体行为界定存在严重分歧。研究表明AI谄媚是一个多样化的行为家族，需要不同的测量、干预和治理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI谄媚缺乏统一定义，现有术语涵盖了从同意用户错误主张到过度赞美、隐瞒纠正反馈等多种行为，导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统对某些谄媚形式仍存在脆弱性。为解决这一概念混乱问题，需要建立统一分类体系和共识。

Method: 1) 综述70篇AI谄媚相关论文，构建行为定义与测量的分类体系；2) 对106位AI谄媚及相关领域专家进行问卷调查，考察研究人员对哪些模型行为属于谄媚的一致程度。

Result: 分类体系区分了两个维度：（1）谄媚针对的是用户立场/信念还是用户个人特质/情感；（2）谄媚通过显性直接语言还是隐性间接行为（如框架、省略、语气）表现。现有文献主要集中于显性的信念导向谄媚，隐性的个人导向行为研究不足。94.3%的专家认为谄媚是重大问题，但对具体行为的界定存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI谄媚是一个涵盖多种行为的广泛家族，不同行为面临不同的测量挑战、干预要求和治理影响。所提出的分类体系为理解和应对这些行为提供了共享词汇，有助于推动评估标准化、缓解策略迁移和政策制定。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [206] [Trace2Skill: Verifier-Guided Skill Evolution for Long-Context EDA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21810)
*Zijian Du,Nathaniel Pinckney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Skill是一种无需微调RTL专用模型、通过将智能体的自然语言技能视为可进化策略，利用 rollout 轨迹挖掘成功/失败模式、结合稠密验证器反馈和进化循环，在复杂Verilog设计问题上显著提升硬件LLM智能体性能的测试时扩展框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂Verilog设计问题（CVDP）对硬件LLM智能体构成巨大挑战，原因在于需要在大规模仓库快照中定位验证器相关的RTL、测试平台、包含路径和构建依赖，进行精确编辑，并从稀疏的隐藏验证器故障中恢复。现有方法要么训练新模型，要么仅采样更多候选解，效率低下且缺乏结构性改进手段。

Method: Trace2Skill将智能体的自然语言技能视为可进化策略。它从重复的 rollout 轨迹中挖掘成功与失败模式，将其转化为密集诊断和预言机经验；然后通过预言器（oracle）、变异器（mutator）和选择器（selector）的循环生成任务特定技能，以指导后续搜索、编辑、验证和恢复。此外，支持有界运行时的稠密验证器反馈，返回经过净化的功能观测结果，同时保持隐藏 harness 和参考解对智能体不可见，从而连接技能文本、验证器证据和下游行为。

Result: 在对抗种子CVDP智能体的困难任务上（包括同样击败前沿编码智能体的任务），Trace2Skill结合稠密验证器反馈显著提高了任务通过率，并在之前未解决的任务上取得了突破性通过。该框架无需高质量微调数据、专用RTL模型训练或模型权重更新。

Conclusion: Trace2Skill提供了一种通用的测试时扩展策略，可超越数字设计领域，扩展到其他可验证的EDA任务。

Abstract: Complex Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) challenge hardware LLM agents because solving them requires localizing verifier-relevant RTL, testbenches, include paths, and build dependencies inside large repository snapshots, making precise edits, and recovering from sparse hidden-verifier failures. We present Trace2Skill, a test-time scaling framework that improves a hardware agent without RTL-specialized model fine-tuning. Rather than training a new model or only sampling more candidate solutions, Trace2Skill treats the agent's natural-language skill as an evolvable policy. It mines repeated rollout traces for success and failure modes, converts them into dense diagnostics and oracle lessons, and uses an oracle, mutator, and selector loop to produce task-specific skills that guide later search, editing, validation, and recovery. Because final pass/fail labels are often too coarse for hard failures, Trace2Skill also supports bounded runtime dense verifier feedback that returns sanitized functional observations while keeping hidden harnesses and reference solutions inaccessible to the agent. This feedback helps guide skill evolution and agent execution by connecting skill text, verifier evidence, and downstream behavior. Across hard CVDP tasks that defeat the seed CVDP agent, including tasks that also defeat frontier coding agents, Trace2Skill with dense verifier feedback substantially improves task pass rates and produces breakthrough passes on previously unsolved tasks, without requiring high-quality fine-tuning data, specialized RTL model training, or model weight updates. The same framework provides a general test-time scaling strategy that can extend beyond digital design to other verifiable EDA tasks.

</details>


### [207] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种从人群偏好中提取共享安全约束的层次化框架Safe Crowd Preference-based RL，用于安全强化学习，无需显式安全奖励即可降低安全成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中的偏好数据隐含了超越任务完成的安全目标，但不同用户的安全偏好存在异质性，需要发现其中共享的安全准则并将其迁移到下游任务中。

Method: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL层次化框架：从人群偏好中提取安全对齐的技能（skills），再通过高层策略组合这些技能以安全地解决下游任务。

Result: 在多个安全RL环境和初步LLM风格任务中，该方法无需显式安全奖励即可显著降低安全成本，任务性能与使用真实安全信号训练的oracle方法相当。

Conclusion: 直接优化偏好学习奖励模型与下游任务奖励的组合存在固有局限，而层次化技能提取与组合方法能有效从人群偏好中发现共享安全准则并迁移到下游任务中。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can reveal implicit objectives such as safety considerations that go beyond task completion. In this work, we focus on the common safety criteria embedded in crowd preference datasets, where different users may express distinct preferences or objectives, yet follow similar safety principles. Our aim is to discover shared safety criteria from crowd preferences and then transfer them to downstream RL tasks to regularize agent behavior and enforce safety. We first show that direct reward combination-optimizing a preference-learned reward model together with downstream task rewards-has inherent limitations. Motivated by this, we propose Safe Crowd Preference-based RL, a hierarchical framework that extracts safety-aligned skills from crowd preferences and composes them via a high-level policy to safely solve downstream tasks. Experiments across safe RL environments and a preliminary LLM-style task with diverse user goals and shared safety constraints demonstrate that our approach substantially lowers safety costs without access to explicit safety rewards, while achieving task performance comparable to oracle methods trained with ground-truth safety signals.

</details>


### [208] [Toward AI VIS Co-Scientists: A General and End-to-End Agent Harness for Solving Complex Data Visualization Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21825)
*Haichao Miao,Zhimin Li,Kuangshi Ai,Kaiyuan Tang,Chaoli Wang,Peer-Timo Bremer,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的智能代理框架，仅基于数据和任务描述即可自主设计定制化的可视化分析应用（VIS Apps），无需领域专家的人工干预，并在IEEE SciVis竞赛数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中，检查、解释和沟通复杂数据的能力至关重要，但通常需要超出核心专业领域的大量专业知识（如数据管理、分析和可视化设计）。现有方法缺乏自动化能力，无法从高层次任务描述直接生成完整的可视化解决方案。

Method: 提出VISCoscientist智能代理框架，包含多个协同工作的代理和专业技能模块：协调探索性分析、规划、环境配置、实现、界面验证以及整体任务完成度评估。每个阶段生成文档和指令产物，指导下游工作并支持迭代优化。

Result: 在IEEE SciVis竞赛数据上验证，系统能够仅凭数据和目标任务自主生成功能完整的单页VIS Apps，具备经过验证的联动视图行为，并为领域专家的特定任务和需求进行高度定制化。

Conclusion: 该工作代表了向通用AI科助（AI co-scientist）迈出的重要一步，即一个能够根据高层指令自主执行长期任务的自治系统，而VIS co-scientist是该愿景中的关键组件。

Abstract: The ability to inspect, interpret, and communicate complex data is crucial for virtually any scientific endeavor, but often requires significant expertise outside the core domain ranging from data management and analysis to visualization design and implementation. We present an end-to-end agentic harness that, based on only the data and a high level description of the tasks, independently designs custom visual analysis applications (VIS apps). This represents an important step towards a general AI co-scientist envisioned by many as an autonomous system that can autonomously execute long horizon tasks based on high-level directions. Our proposed VIS co-scientist is an essential component of this broader AI co-scientist vision: a harness that can autonomously analyze data and design visualization solutions using a collection of agents and specialized skills that coordinate exploratory analysis, plan, configure the environment, implement, validate the interface, and most importantly evaluate the overall task completion. Each stage produces document and instruction artifacts that guide downstream work and enable iterative refinement. We validate this approach on IEEE SciVis Contests spanning multiple science and engineering fields. These contests serve as ideal proving grounds because they encode real-world complexity: ambiguous requirements, diverse data modalities, design trade-offs, and task-driven validation. Given only the data and target tasks, our system autonomously produces functional single-page VIS Apps with verified linked-view behavior, highly customized to domain experts' specified tasks and needs.

</details>


### [209] [FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21832)
*Xinhang Yuan,Zexi Huang,Anjia Cao,Xudong Lu,Zikai Wang,Penghao Zhou,Chang Liu,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLUID提出首个完全移除物品ID的直播推荐排序框架，通过跨域多模态编码器生成离散层次化编码（LUCID）替代ID嵌入，在超十亿用户规模的工业场景中显著提升冷启动直播推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播推荐中，直播间仅持续数十分钟，其物品ID处于持续冷启动状态，基于ID的协同过滤模型难以泛化，亟需无需ID的排序方案。

Method: 采用跨域多模态编码器（联合训练短视频和直播内容）生成离散层次化编码LUCID；提出后融合、无ID设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立token注入；通过分阶段预热策略稳定在线增量训练。

Result: 在超十亿全球用户工业级直播推荐系统中，Quality Watch Duration提升0.55%，冷启动房间观看量提升2.05%，Active Hours提升0.05%。

Conclusion: FLUID完全摆脱ID依赖，为直播推荐中的冷启动问题提供了可工业部署的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely heavily on ID-based collaborative filtering: each item is represented by a unique ID embedding that accumulates collaborative signals from user interactions. Livestreaming recommendation, however, faces a unique challenge in this paradigm: a live room typically broadcasts for only tens of minutes, so its item ID remains poorly learned in a persistent cold-start state and ID-centric ranking models fail to generalize. We present FLUID, the first framework to fully retire the candidate-side item ID from a production-scale livestreaming ranker. FLUID couples a cross-domain multimodal encoder, jointly trained on short videos and livestreams to produce discrete hierarchical codes (LUCID), with a late-fusion, ID-free design that injects slice-level and room-level LUCID as independent tokens, stabilized by a staged warmup under online incremental training. Deployed on our industrial livestreaming recommenders with a cross-platform combined user base of over one billion globally, FLUID delivers significant online gains of +0.55% Quality Watch Duration, +2.05% Cold-Start Room Views, and +0.05% Active Hours.

</details>


### [210] [Planning in the LLM Era: Building for Reliability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21902)
*Michael Katz,Harsha Kokel,Kavitha Srinivas,Shirin Sohrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文梳理了LLM在规划领域的演变趋势：从直接生成计划，到结合外部搜索的混合方法，再到当前利用LLM生成可验证的符号求解器，旨在实现推理时的高效与可靠。作者将这一转变视为LLM时代规划领域的重大重新对齐，并分析了三类规划器生成方法及其局限，提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能代理的核心能力之一是规划。早期利用LLM进行单次规划生成和混合搜索方法存在不完备、资源消耗大、泛化性差等问题。随着LLM局限性的显现，需要探索更可靠、资源高效的规划方法，促使研究转向利用LLM生成可验证的符号求解器，以减少推理时对语言模型的依赖。

Method: 本文采用综述与前瞻性分析的方法，将LLM时代的规划器生成方法归纳为三大类别，分析各类方法的特点、优势和局限性，并基于现有趋势提出未来研究路线。

Result: 识别出规划器生成方法的三种主要类别；指出该趋势反映了规划领域在LLM时代的一次更广泛的重新对齐；揭示了当前方法在可靠性和效率方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 利用LLM在规划生成阶段（而非推理阶段）构建可验证的符号求解器，是实现可靠且资源高效智能代理的有效路径。未来研究应致力于减少推理时对LLM的依赖，提升规划器的可维护性与生成效率。

Abstract: Growing attention to intelligent agents has put a spotlight on one of their central capabilities: planning. Early attempts to leverage large language models (LLMs) for planning relied on single-shot plan generation, followed by hybrid approaches that coupled LLMs with limited external search. These methods, unsound and incomplete by their very nature, often require substantial resources without yielding better solutions on unseen problems. As the limitations of LLMs become clearer, recent work has shifted toward using them at solution construction time -- generating symbolic solvers for a family of problems that can be verified and then used efficiently at inference time. This trend reflects the growing need for agents that are both reliable and resource-efficient. It also offers a path towards generating maintainable planners with minimal dependence on language models at inference time. In this paper, we argue that this shift reflects a broader realignment of the planning field in the LLM era. We examine three major categories of planner-generation methods, discuss their current limitations, and outline research steps towards a more reliable and efficient LLM-based generation of planners.

</details>


### [211] [AI-Enabled Serious Games: Integrating Intelligence and Adaptivity in Training Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21962)
*Priyamvada Tripathi,Bill Kapralos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本章探讨了如何通过AI技术（如大语言模型、强化学习、智能体架构）实现严肃游戏中的实时教学适应，区分了教学智能与适应性，并综述了从早期计算机辅助教学到AI驱动系统的演进，同时指出了可解释性、验证、计算成本等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严肃游戏在静态场景设计、创作瓶颈、学习者建模有限以及难以实现有意义的实时教学适应等方面存在持续挑战，亟需利用AI新能力加以解决。

Method: 通过历史综述梳理从计算机辅助教学、智能辅导系统、动态难度调整、创作平台、学习分析到AI架构的演进，并分析LLM、强化学习和智能体架构如何贡献于游戏中的教学智能与适应性。

Result: AI技术（LLM、RL、智能体架构）能够支持动态场景变化、上下文反馈、自适应节奏和学习者状态建模，有望应对严肃游戏的现有局限。

Conclusion: AI集成严肃游戏面临可解释性、验证、计算成本及长期学习效果实证证据不足等挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Serious games are widely used for learning and training across domains such as healthcare, defense, and education. Persistent challenges remain, however, including static scenario design, authoring bottlenecks, limited learner modeling, and difficulty implementing meaningful real-time instructional adaptation. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) introduce novel capabilities such as dynamic scenario variation, contextual feedback, adaptive pacing, and learner-state modeling that may help address some of these limitations. At the same time, integrating AI into serious games raises important questions related to validity, transparency, system control, and learner trust. This chapter examines how contemporary AI approaches may support real-time instructional adaptation in serious games. It distinguishes between instructional intelligence, defined as a system's capacity to infer learner knowledge and reason about pedagogically appropriate responses, and adaptivity, defined as the ability to modify instructional actions during interaction. A historical synthesis of adaptive learning systems is presented, tracing developments from early computer-assisted instruction through intelligent tutoring systems (ITS), dynamic difficulty adjustment (DDA), authoring platforms, learning analytics, and recent AI-enabled architectures. Building on this perspective, the chapter discusses how large language models (LLMs), reinforcement learning (RL), and agent-based architectures may contribute to more integrated forms of intelligence and adaptivity in serious games. It also highlights practical and research challenges associated with AI-enabled systems, including explainability, validation, computational cost, and the limited empirical evidence regarding long-term learning outcomes in AI-enabled serious games.

</details>


### [212] [Format-Constraint Coupling in Knowledge Graph Construction from Statistical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21974)
*Jingxuan Qi,Zhiqiang Ye,Yuxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在统计CSV数据上，序列化格式与抽取模式约束之间的交互效应（格式化约束耦合），发现二者的联合效应超过独立效应之和，可能导致知识图谱事实覆盖率的严重下降。通过实验验证了表面形式锚定解释，并发现直接图访问比标准检索模式更能暴露构建质量问题。发布了包含15个数据集、1,892个黄金标准事实的CSVFidelity-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱抽取模式在CSV数据上的应用可能降低事实保真度，尤其是在序列化格式与模式约束不匹配时会导致灾难性后果。现有评估方法（标准检索模式）掩盖了构建质量问题，亟需更有效的诊断手段和标准化基准。

Method: 采用2x2析因实验设计（6个数据集），分析序列化格式与模式约束的交互效应；通过探测分析和词元消融实验验证表面形式锚定解释；对比三种标准检索模式与直接图访问的诊断效果；构建CSVFidelity-Bench基准（15个数据集、6个领域、1,892个事实）。

Result: 格式与模式的联合效应超过独立效应之和，最大达+1.180（2x2析因实验）；4/6数据集的Bootstrap 95%置信区间严格为正，宽矩阵（Type-II）证据最强；不匹配组合导致事实覆盖率低于无约束基线（4/6数据集）；三种标准检索模式掩盖质量差异（delta ≤ 1pp），直接图访问暴露高达+47.6pp的差距（p < 0.0001）。

Conclusion: 提出了格式-约束耦合（format-constraint coupling）现象，揭示了序列化格式与模式约束的交互效应会降低知识图谱保真度；推荐使用直接图访问进行保真度诊断；CSVFidelity-Bench为相关研究提供了标准化评估工具。

Abstract: An extraction schema should not reduce knowledge graph fidelity. On statistical CSV, however, it can. We study country-by-year time-series matrices, a common layout on open-data portals. In this setting, serialization format and schema constraints interact super-additively. Their joint effect exceeds the sum of independent effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial, 6 datasets). Bootstrap 95% CIs are strictly positive on 4/6 datasets, with strongest evidence on wide Type-II matrices. More critically, a schema applied to a mismatched format can trigger catastrophic mismatch. Fact coverage falls below the unconstrained baseline on 4/6 datasets through entity inflation or extraction refusal. We call this observed pattern format-constraint coupling. Probing and token ablation support a surface-form anchoring explanation centred on column-name references. Controlled variants across format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families show the same direction within the measured scope; one LLM family shows only partial activation. The observation also has a diagnostic consequence. Three standard retrieval modes largely mask construction quality (delta <= 1pp), whereas direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp (p < 0.0001). To support fidelity-aware evaluation, we release CSVFidelity-Bench. It contains 15 datasets, 11 Type-II matrices, 4 Type-III tables, and 1,892 Gold Standard facts across 6 domains.

</details>


### [213] [Echo: Learning from Experience Data via User-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21984)
*Hande Dong,Xiaoyun Liang,Jiarui Yu,Jiayi Lin,Changqing Ai,Feng Liu,Wenjun Zhang,Rongbi Wei,Chaofan Zhu,Linjie Che,Feng Wu,Xin Shen,Dexu Kong,Xiaotian Wang,Qiuyuan Chen,Bingxu An,Yueting Lei,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Echo框架，通过利用用户对智能体输出进行精炼产生的序列数据，将原始交互经验转化为高质量训练信号，从而实现智能体的持续对齐和性能提升。在代码补全场景中，该框架将接受率从25.7%提升至35.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态人类数据存在扩展成本高且受限于创建者知识的问题，而原始交互日志噪声大、信息密度低，难以直接用于模型训练。因此需要一种方法将原始经验数据转化为可学习的知识。

Method: Echo框架通过捕获用户对智能体输出的精炼过程（用户驱动精炼序列），将智能体的粗糙尝试转化为高质量训练信号，并将其反馈到训练循环中实现模型持续优化。

Result: 在生产级代码补全环境中，该框架将代码建议接受率从25.7%提升至35.7%，打破了静态性能天花板。

Conclusion: Echo框架有效实现了从原始经验到可学习知识的转换，通过用户精炼信号持续对齐智能体与真实需求，证明了经验数据驱动范式超越静态数据限制的可行性。

Abstract: Static "human data" faces inherent limitations: it is expensive to scale and bounded by the knowledge of its creators. Continuous learning from "experience data" - interactions between agents and their environments - promises to transcend these barriers. Today, the widespread deployment of AI agents grants us low-cost access to massive streams of such real-world experience. However, raw interaction logs are inherently noisy, filled with trial-and-error and low information density, rendering them inefficient for direct model training.
  We introduce Echo, a generalized framework designed to operationalize the transition from raw experience to learnable knowledge, effectively "echoing" environmental feedback back into the training loop for model optimization. In today's agent ecosystem, user refinement serves as a primary source of such feedback: driven by responsibility for the outcome, users rigorously transform flawed agent proposals into verified solutions. These user-driven refinement sequences inherently distill agents' crude attempts into high-quality training signals. Echo systematically harvests these signals to continuously align the agent with real-world needs. Large-scale validation in a production code completion environment confirms that Echo effectively harnesses this pipeline, breaking the static performance ceiling by increasing the acceptance rate from 25.7% to 35.7%.

</details>


### [214] [ECPO: Evidence-Coupled Policy Optimization for Evidence-Certified Candidate Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21993)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,BoKun Wang,Yina Sa,Xin Wang,Xiaobo Guo,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出证据认证候选排序任务及证据耦合策略优化(ECPO)方法，在排序同时输出可验证的证据引证，通过列表级策略优化联合优化排序质量与证据有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策支持场景中的排序系统不仅需要排序候选，还应提供可独立验证的证据，确保决策过程透明可信。

Method: 提出ECPO框架：1) 从骨架对齐、论元一致性和图特征学习可解释轨迹奖励；2) 使用三项耦合奖励（列表级排序效用、跨度级证书有效性、无标签确定性验证器的证据循环奖励）优化约束策略；3) 通过CertNDCG指标联合评估排序与证据质量。

Result: 在MAVEN-ERE和RAMS数据集上，将ECPO与零样本、SFT、GRPO策略、奖励模型排序、约束解码、验证器重试等多种基线方法进行对比，在闭集、预测集和混合集三种候选池设置下验证效果。

Conclusion: 将排序目标从仅优化NDCG扩展为优化CertNDCG和决策-证据耦合，为可信排序系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Ranking systems used in decision-support settings should not only order candidates but also expose evidence that can be independently checked. We study evidence-certified candidate ranking: given an intent_id, a predefined plan skeleton, a window-local candidate roster, and text-derived candidate trajectories with span provenance, a system must output a Top-K list together with doc_id:span evidence certificates whose cited spans are sufficient to recover the decision. We instantiate this task on MAVEN-ERE and RAMS with fixed upstream extraction, window-local randomized candidate identifiers, skeleton-aligned trajectory supervision, hard negatives, and audit references. We introduce Evidence-Coupled Policy Optimization (ECPO), a listwise policy-optimization objective whose action is the joint object of ranking and evidence certificate. ECPO first learns an interpretable trajectory reward from skeleton alignment, argument consistency, and optional graph features; it then optimizes a constrained policy with three coupled rewards: listwise ranking utility, span-level certificate validity, and an evidence-cycle reward computed by a label-free deterministic verifier that reconstructs candidate support from claim-stripped cited spans. This reframes the goal from maximizing ordinary NDCG alone to maximizing CertNDCG and decision-evidence coupling. The evaluation compares ECPO against zero-shot, SFT, and GRPO policies, RM-only scoring with deterministic evidence attachment, grammar/JSON-constrained decoding, validator retry, best-of-N RM selection, and post-hoc evidence rationalization under closed-roster, predicted-roster, and hybrid-roster settings.

</details>


### [215] [The Log is the Agent: Event-Sourced Reactive Graphs for Auditable, Forkable Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21997)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActiveGraph 是一个倒置传统 agent 框架的运行时：以仅追加事件日志为真相源，工作图是日志的确定性投影，行为通过响应图变化发射新事件来实现协调，而非组件间直接指令。这带来了确定性重放、低成本分叉和端到端 lineage 三大特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 框架以语言模型对话循环为中心，工具、规则、日志和记忆系统是后加的，导致状态持久化依赖可检索的"记忆"，缺乏确定性重放、因果可追溯等能力。

Method: 设计 ActiveGraph 运行时，核心原则是：仅追加事件日志为唯一真相源；工作图作为日志的确定性投影；行为（普通函数、类、LLM 例程或类型化边逻辑）通过响应图变化发射新事件来间接协调，所有组件通过共享图进行通信而非直接指令。

Result: 该设计实现三个检索-摘要记忆系统无法提供的特性：1) 从日志确定性重放任意运行；2) 低成本分叉，可在任意事件处分支运行而无需重新执行共享前缀；3) 从高层目标到产生每个工件具体模型调用的端到端 lineage。

Conclusion: ActiveGraph 为自改进 agent 提供了良好的基础架构，能够从日志中完整重建因果结构，延续了 BabyAGI 和之前图记忆研究的路线。

Abstract: Most agent frameworks are built around the language model: a conversation loop comes first, then tools, then rules, and finally a logging layer bolted on for observability, with state persisted as retrievable "memory." We describe ActiveGraph, a runtime that inverts this arrangement. The append-only event log is the source of truth; the working graph is a deterministic projection of that log; and behaviors--ordinary functions, classes, LLM-backed routines, or logic attached to typed edges--react to changes in the graph and emit new events. No component instructs another; coordination happens entirely through the shared graph. This single design decision yields three properties that retrieval-and-summarization memory systems do not provide: deterministic replay of any run from its log, cheap forking that branches a run at any event without re-executing the shared prefix, and end-to-end lineage from a high-level goal down to the individual model call that produced each artifact. We present the architecture, a determinism contract that makes replay sound, and a worked diligence example whose full causal structure is reconstructable from the log alone. We discuss--without claiming to demonstrate--why this substrate is unusually well suited to self-improving agents, and how it extends the BabyAGI lineage and prior graph-memory research.

</details>


### [216] [Active Evidence-Seeking and Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models for Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22047)
*Chen Zhan,Xihe Qiu,Xiaoyu Tan,Xibing Zhuang,Gengchen Ma,Yue Zhang,Shuo Li,Peifeng Liu,Xiaoxiao Ge,Liang Liu,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于OSCE的标准化病人模拟器用于主动诊断询问的交互评估基准，发现相比全上下文评估，多轮证据获取使诊断准确率下降12.75%，证据质量下降24.36%，错误主要源于过早诊断闭合和低效提问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在静态医学考试中表现良好，但临床诊断需要在不确定性下进行迭代证据收集，现有评估方法无法反映这种交互式诊断能力。

Method: 构建OSCE启发的标准化病人模拟器及可重复基准；在468个病例和15个模型上，比较多轮主动询问与全上下文静态评估的诊断表现。

Result: 多轮证据获取使诊断准确率下降12.75%，支持证据质量下降24.36%；错误与过早诊断闭合和低效提问相关。

Conclusion: 静态全上下文基准可能高估模型在交互式证据获取场景中的表现，需要补充交互式评估以实现更安全的临床决策支持。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on static medical examinations, yet clinical diagnosis often requires iterative evidence gathering under uncertainty. Building on prior interactive evaluation efforts, we introduce an OSCE-inspired standardized patient simulator and a controlled, reproducible benchmark for active diagnostic inquiry. Across 468 cases and 15 models in our protocol, we observe that multi-turn evidence seeking reduces diagnostic accuracy by 12.75% and lowers supporting-evidence quality by 24.36% relative to full-context evaluation; error analyses associate these drops with premature diagnostic closure and inefficient questioning. Together, these results suggest that static full-context benchmarks may overestimate performance in interactive evidence-seeking settings, motivating complementary interactive assessment for safer clinical decision support.

</details>


### [217] [Enhancing Visual Token Representations for Video Large Language Models via Training-Free Spatial-Temporal Pooling and Gridding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22078)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Hanqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ST-GridPool，一种无需训练的视觉token增强方法，通过金字塔时序网格化（PTG）捕获多粒度时空交互，以及基于范数的空间池化（NSP）保留高信息区域，显著提升视频大语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM（如LLaVA系列）在视频理解中采用简单的池化或插值技术处理视觉token，忽略了视觉token的复杂动态特性，无法高效压缩视觉token同时保留时空交互信息。

Method: 提出ST-GridPool，包含两个核心组件：1）Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG) — 通过分层时序网格化捕获多粒度时空交互；2）Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP) — 利用token范数与语义丰富度的相关性，保留高信息的视觉区域。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，ST-GridPool无需昂贵的重新训练即可持续提升视频大语言模型的性能。

Conclusion: ST-GridPool是一种高效、即插即用的视频LLM视觉token表示改进方案，无需训练即可增强模型性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced video understanding tasks, yet challenges remain in efficiently compressing visual tokens while preserving spatiotemporal interactions. Existing methods, such as LLaVA family, utilize simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens.
