Most existing spreadsheet agents rely on specialized prompting over general-purpose LLMs; while this design has potentials on simple spreadsheet operations, it struggles to manage the complex, multi-step workflows typical of real-world applications.
  We introduce Spreadsheet-RL, a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework designed to train specialized spreadsheet agents within a realistic Microsoft Excel environment. Spreadsheet-RL features an automated pipeline for scalable collection of paired start-goal spreadsheets from online forums, as well as domain-specific evaluation tasks in areas such as finance and supply chain management, which we compile into the new Domain-Spreadsheet benchmark dataset. It also includes a Spreadsheet Gym environment designed for multi-turn RL: Spreadsheet Gym exposes extensive Excel functionality through a Python sandbox, along with a refined harness that incorporates a comprehensive tool set and carefully designed tool-routing rules for spreadsheet tasks. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that Spreadsheet-RL substantially enhances AI agent's performance on both general and domain-specific spreadsheet tasks: it improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507's Pass@1 on SpreadsheetBench from 12.0% to 23.4%, and raises Pass@1 from 8.4% to 17.2% on our curated Domain-Spreadsheet dataset. These results highlight Spreadsheet-RL's strong potential for generalization and real-world adoption in spreadsheet automation, and broadly, its promise for advancing LLM-based interactions with data interfaces in everyday work.

</details>


### [254] [AtelierEval: Agentic Evaluation of Humans & LLMs as Text-to-Image Prompters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22645)
*Hanjun Luo,Zhimu Huang,Sylvia Chung,Yiran Wang,Yingbin Jin,Jialin Li,Jiang Li,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtelierEval是首个统一量化评估提示者（人类或MLLM）提示能力的基准，包含360个专家设计的任务；AtelierJudge是一种基于技能、记忆增强的评估器，可对提示-图像对进行主客观评分，Spearman相关系数达0.79，实验揭示了模仿优于规划的现象，并倡导未来提示者向图像增强方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像（T2I）系统的基准仅评估T2I模型本身，而忽略了上游提示者（人类或多模态大语言模型）将用户意图转化为详细提示的能力，缺乏对这一关键环节的量化评估工具。

Method: 构建了AtelierEval统一基准，基于认知视角涵盖三大任务类别，利用真实世界挑战分类法实例化360个专家设计任务，支持人类和MLLM双接口；提出AtelierJudge评估器，采用基于技能和记忆增强的智能体方法对提示-图像对进行主客观评分。

Result: AtelierJudge与人类专家的Spearman相关系数达0.79，接近人类水平；在4个T2I后端上对8个MLLM和48名人类用户进行了广泛实验，验证了AtelierEval作为诊断工具的有效性，并揭示了模仿策略优于规划策略，建议未来提示者采用图像增强方向。

Conclusion: AtelierEval可作为评估提示者能力的可靠诊断工具，揭示了当前提示策略的局限性，为未来提示者设计指明了图像增强的发展方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems increasingly rely on upstream prompters, either humans or multimodal large language models (MLLMs), to translate user intent into detailed prompts. Yet current benchmarks fix the prompt and only evaluate T2I models, leaving the prompting proficiency of this upstream component entirely unmeasured. We introduce AtelierEval, the first unified benchmark that quantifies prompting proficiency across 360 expert-crafted tasks. Grounded in a cognitive view, it spans three task categories and instantiates tasks using a taxonomy of real-world challenges, with a dual interface for both humans and MLLMs. To enable scalable and reliable evaluation, we propose AtelierJudge, a skill-based, memory-augmented agentic evaluator. It produces subjective and objective scores for prompt-image pairs, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.79 with human experts, approaching human performance. Extensive experiments benchmark 8 MLLMs against 48 human users across 4 T2I backends, validate AtelierEval as a robust diagnostic tool, and reveal the superiority of mimicry over planning, advocating for an image-augmented direction for future prompters. Our work is released to support future research.

</details>


### [255] [Claw AI Lab: An Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22662)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Chen,Zhenshan Tan,Taiyu Zhang,Xinzhen Xu,Yanyu Qian,Dingcheng Gao,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Yi Tan,Deyi Ji,Guosheng Lin,Tianrun Chen,Deheng Ye,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw AI Lab是一个实验室原生的自主研究平台，它从隐藏的提示到论文的流水线转变为交互式AI实验室。用户可以通过一个提示实例化完整的研究团队，支持可定制的角色、协作工作流、实时监控、工件检查和回滚/恢复控制。平台包含Claw-Code Harness连接本地代码库、数据集和检查点，提高了实验完整性和结果可靠性。在五个AI研究案例的内部评估中，相比AutoResearchClaw基线，Claw AI Lab在创意新颖性、实验完整性和论文呈现质量上获得AI专家评审的一致偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动研究系统通常是隐藏的提示到论文流水线，缺乏交互性、可控性和实验室般的实践体验。用户无法灵活配置研究角色、监控过程或检查中间工件，导致研究过程不透明且难以迭代。

Method: Claw AI Lab采用多智能体研究团队架构，用户通过单一提示即可实例化完整研究团队，支持可定制角色和协作工作流。平台提供统一仪表盘实现实时监控、工件检查和回滚/恢复控制。核心组件Claw-Code Harness连接本地代码库、数据集和检查点，使可执行实验与工件反馈形成闭环。平台还支持探索、多智能体讨论和复现三种研究模式。

Result: 在五个AI研究案例的内部评估中，以AutoResearchClaw为基线，Claw AI Lab在创意新颖性、实验完整性和论文呈现质量上均获得AI专家评审的一致偏好。Claw-Code Harness改善了执行集成、实验完成度和结果完整性，减少了部分运行和格式错误的报告等常见失败模式。

Conclusion: Claw AI Lab代表了自主研究向可用、交互式和可靠性感知的科学基础设施转变的早期一步，开创了实验室式自主研究的新范式。

Abstract: We present Claw AI Lab, a lab-native autonomous research platform that advances automated research from a hidden prompt-to-paper pipeline into an interactive AI laboratory. Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [256] [WorkstreamBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664)
*Thomson Yen,Julian Poeltl,Harshith Srinivas Gear,Yilin Meng,Joshua Fan,Adam Shen,Yili Liu,Ali Bauyrzhan,Siri Du,Haoyang Liu,Daniel Guetta,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向端到端电子表格任务的智能体评测基准，聚焦金融建模与场景分析等经济关键工作流。开发了包含准确性、公式、格式三个维度的评估体系。实验表明，Claude系列表现最佳，但即使最强智能体在复杂任务中仍远低于专业金融标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格基准仅测评问答或单公式编辑等简单能力，无法衡量智能体从零构建完整电子表格（如金融建模、预测、场景分析）的高级能力，存在明显评估空白。

Method: 1) 聚焦金融工作流（建模、场景分析）的端到端电子表格任务；2) 构建三维评估体系：准确性（数值正确性）、公式（计算逻辑合理性）、格式（可读性与易修改性）；3) 包含难度分级的任务集与定性评审。

Result: Claude系列在基准中领先且输出最具专业性外观；但所有智能体在难度超出简单链式计算后性能急剧下降，整体远未达到专业金融标准。

Conclusion: 当前智能体尚不能可靠地生成达到真实工作流复杂度要求的专业级电子表格，尤其在多维质量标准下表现不足。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected to carry out end-to-end workflows, producing complete artifacts from high-level user instructions. To meet enterprise needs, frontier AI labs have developed agents that can construct entire spreadsheets from scratch. This is especially relevant in finance, where core workflows such as financial modeling, forecasting, and scenario analysis are commonly conducted through spreadsheets. Yet, existing spreadsheet benchmarks do not measure this advanced capability, focusing instead on question-answering or single-formula edits. To address this gap, we provide one of the first evaluations of agents on end-to-end spreadsheet tasks, focusing on economically critical financial workflows such as modeling and scenario analysis. Since deliverables therein are routinely reviewed and revised by multiple stakeholders, judging their quality necessarily involves high-level criteria such as readability or ease of modification. To reflect the multidimensional nature of solution quality, we develop an evaluation taxonomy comprising three dimensions: Accuracy, Formula, and Format, each comprising fine-grained criteria that reflect professional standards. The Claude family leads the benchmark and produces the most professional-looking outputs in our qualitative review, but even the strongest agents frequently fall short of professional finance standards and degrade sharply as the difficulty increases beyond a few chained calculations. This suggests that current agents are not yet able to reliably produce professional-quality spreadsheets at the level of complexity real-world workflows demand.

</details>


### [257] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在具有超线性增长和体制转换尾部风险的预测任务（如金融和流行病学）中，LLM存在逆缩放现象：越强大的模型做出越差的分位数预测。这一现象在合成基准FBSim、真实COVID-19/麻疹/房价/恶性通胀数据中均成立，且集中于上尾分布。单阈值指标会掩盖该问题，建议采用连续无界精度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测基准多使用单阈值指标（如分类准确率），可能掩盖模型在尾部风险预测中的系统性失败。作者希望揭示大模型在超线性增长与体制转换场景下的能力退化现象，并指出当前评估指标的缺陷。

Method: 构建无污染模拟基准FBSim（合成SIR流行病+线性对照），并在COVID-19、麻疹、房价、恶性通胀等真实数据集上验证。采用分位数分解分析上下尾差异，在Llama-3.1系列内进行跨规模与后训练对比研究，对比单阈值与连续精度指标的效果。

Result: 逆缩放现象在超线性增长任务中显著：越大模型上尾预测越激进、校准越差；后训练和规模扩大均独立贡献该效应；领域知识无法可靠修复校准；单阈值指标（如分类准确率）完全掩盖逆缩放，甚至反转能力-准确度关系符号。

Conclusion: LLM预测评估应包含连续无界精度指标（如分位数损失）以捕获尾部风险，而非仅依赖有界二元阈值指标；当前LLM预测能力评估可能系统性高估模型在极端事件上的表现。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [258] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CUSP基准，通过4760个科学事件系统评估AI系统预测科学进展的能力，发现当前前沿模型在可行性评估、机制推理、解决方案设计和时间预测等方面存在系统性局限，无法可靠预测科学进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI是否能够预测科学进展，这是一个尚未厘清的问题。现有研究缺乏时间锚定的评估框架来检验AI在受控知识约束下预测科学进步的能力。

Method: 构建CUSP（Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress）基准，采用时间锚定的评估框架，从可行性评估、机制推理、生成性方案设计、时间预测四个维度，在4760个科学事件上评估前沿AI模型的表现。

Result: 模型能识别竞争候选方向中的合理路径，但无法可靠预测科学进步是否会发生，且系统性错误估计时间。AI领域的时间可预测性高于生物、化学和物理。模型表现对事件发生在训练截止点前后不敏感，额外知识能提升表现但无法弥补与全信息设置的差距。模型存在系统性过度自信和响应偏差。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统作为科学进展预测工具存在明显不足，先验知识无法转化为可靠预测能力，模型更多受益于事后信息而非前瞻性预测。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [259] [Beyond the Org Chart: AI and the Transformation of Invisible Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22707)
*Stephanie Rosenthal,Shamsi Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对24名产品导向员工的访谈，研究了AI如何改变大型科技公司的工作流程、角色职责、团队协作以及非正式文化实践（如职业指导），发现AI既促进了协作，也威胁了传统职业发展机会，并提出了使隐形工作可见化及维护健康公司文化的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在AI优先的公司中，工作流程如何变化，特别是AI如何影响正式角色职责、团队协作以及非正式文化实践（如职业指导）。

Method: 对一家大型科技公司的24名产品导向员工进行访谈，了解AI如何影响他们自身工作、产品团队内的工作以及职业互动。

Result: AI不仅改变了正式角色职责和协作方式，还改变了职业指导等非正式文化实践；协作变得更顺畅，但传统职业成长机会（如通过专业网络获得反馈、促进领导力和指导）面临风险。

Conclusion: AI公司应采取措施使隐形工作更可见，个人和领导者应支持同事度过AI转型，同时维护支持多元思维、协作和非正式互动的健康公司文化。

Abstract: An increasing number of news and research articles report that AI adoption is allowing professionals to blur and extend the boundaries of their corporate roles. With the goal of understanding how work processes might be changing in an AI-forward company, we interviewed 24 product-focused individuals at a large technology firm about how AI has impacted their own work, their work within their product team, and their professional interactions. Our conversations suggest that AI is not only changing formal role responsibilities and collaborations between those roles, but also changing informal cultural practices like professional mentoring that are key to helping professionals settle in their positions, stay engaged with their work, and grow their careers. Some of these changes are positive, such as smoother collaboration between peers, but other changes are more nuanced and put the typical career growth opportunities, like receiving feedback from professional networks and promoting leadership and mentorship, at risk. We propose steps that AI companies can take to make the invisible work more visible. Additionally, we propose efforts that individuals and leaders can take to support their colleagues through AI transformation while preserving healthy company cultures that support diverse thinking, collaboration, and informal interactions.

</details>


### [260] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM作为自动评估器时，对话历史中的情感极性（正面/负面）会系统性偏置后续判断（即AMEL效应），该效应在模型不确定性高时更强，负面历史偏置是正面的1.62倍，且不随上下文长度增加而恶化，但可通过每次重新创建上下文来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用作自动评估器（如审核代码、内容审核、评分输出），且通常在同一对话中处理大量项目。作者质疑历史对话的极性（正面/负面）是否会偏置后续判断，即探究对话历史累积的情感信号是否影响LLM评估的客观性。

Method: 在75,898次API调用中，对11种模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及4个开源模型）进行实验，将相同的测试项目以隔离方式呈现，或置于以正面/负面评价为主的历史对话之后。通过熵分析区分高不确定性项目与确定性项目，并测试不同历史长度（5轮vs 50轮）、不同位置（固定位置vs随机位置）及不同极性比例的影响。

Result: 模型判断会向对话历史的主流极性偏移（d = -0.17, p < 10^-46）。该效应在高熵（模型不确定）项目上更强（d = -0.34 vs d = -0.15）。偏置不随上下文长度增长（5轮与50轮无显著差异）。负面历史带来的偏置是正面历史的1.62倍（t = 13.46, p < 10^-39）。缩放模型规模可缓解但无法消除（如Haiku -0.22到Opus -0.17；Nano -0.34到GPT-5.2 -0.17）。token概率分布是连续偏移而非阈值突变。负面不对称性同时具有token级和语义成分。偏置历史的位置无关紧要。

Conclusion: LLM评估存在AMEL效应，即对话历史极性偏置后续判断。最简单的修复方案是每个项目使用新的上下文；当必须批处理时，平衡历史中的极性有助于减轻偏置。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [261] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了参数化模块逻辑程序这一新形式化框架，用于实现一阶回答集编程（ASP）中的模块化，该框架支持带参数和内涵性声明的子程序定义，能够捕获clingo程序中集体控制特性的语义，并建立了模块化ASP与传统非模块化ASP之间的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶回答集编程缺乏系统的模块化机制，导致大型程序难以结构化组织和复用。本文旨在填补这一空白，为ASP引入正式的模块化理论框架。

Method: 提出了参数化模块逻辑程序（Parametric Modular Logic Programs）的形式化框架，通过引入参数和内涵性声明（intensionality statements）来定义子程序模块，并形式化地证明了该框架能够捕获clingo中集体控制（collective control）特性的语义。

Result: 成功建立了模块化ASP的理论基础，证明了所提形式化框架与clingo-programs中集体控制特性的语义等价性，并展示了其与传统非模块化ASP之间的理论连接。

Conclusion: 模块化ASP通过参数化模块逻辑程序得到严格的形式化定义，不仅增强了ASP程序的结构化能力，还保持了与传统ASP语义的一致性，为实际ASP开发提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [262] [Can AI Make Conflicts Worse? An Alignment Failure in LLM Deployment Across Conflict Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22720)
*Andrii Kryshtal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统评估了来自OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI四个提供商的九种模型配置在武装冲突背景下的表现，发现模型在虚假对等、否认种族灭绝、未能识别种族侮辱等关键问题上存在6%至47%的失败率，当用户要求"平衡"时，五种配置的失败率高达80-100%。研究发布了首个针对该领域的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型已在受武装冲突影响的社会中部署，记者、人道主义工作者、政府和普通公民依赖它们获取信息或用于工作流程，但目前尚无既定实践来检查其输出是否会加剧冲突。

Method: 对四个提供商（OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI）的九种模型配置进行了90个多轮场景测试，旨在发现冲突背景下（如虚假对等、否认种族灭绝、未能识别种族侮辱等）的失调行为。

Result: 最佳与最差模型之间的失败率范围为6%至47%；当用户要求在国际法院已确认责任的问题上保持"平衡"时，五种配置在80-100%的情况下失败。

Conclusion: 模型选择本身即是一个安全问题，应将该评估框架纳入对齐评估组合中。

Abstract: AI models are already deployed in societies affected by armed conflict, and journalists, humanitarian workers, governments and ordinary citizens rely on them for information or for their work processes. No established practice exists for checking whether their outputs can make those conflicts worse. We tested nine model configurations from four providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI) on 90 multi-turn scenarios designed to surface misaligned behaviour in conflict contexts: false equivalence between documented atrocities, denial of genocide, and failure to recognise ethnic slurs, among others. When such outputs feed into journalism, humanitarian reporting, or public debate, they can deepen divisions in fragile societies. Failure rates span 6\% to 47\% between the best and worst performing models, which makes model choice a safety question in its own right and when users pushed for ``balance'' in cases where international courts have already assigned responsibility, five of nine configurations failed 80 to 100 percent of the time. We release the first evaluation framework for this domain and propose adding it to alignment evaluation portfolios.

</details>


### [263] [Beyond Acoustic Emotion Recognition: Multimodal Pathos Analysis in Political Speech Using LLM-Based and Acoustic Emotion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22732)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了声学情感识别模型（emotion2vec）与多模态LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash、TRUST管线）在政治演讲Pathos维度分析中的表现，发现LLM在语义级政治情感捕捉上显著优于纯声学模型，但声学特征对低层唤醒度估计仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究声学情感识别模型能否作为政治演讲分析中Pathos维度的代理指标，评估不同模态（纯声学 vs 多模态LLM）在政治情感分析中的有效性。

Method: 以Bundestag某议员演讲（51个片段，245秒）为案例，比较三种分析模态：(1) emotion2vec_plus_large声学SER模型（通过Russell Circumplex投影导出连续Arousal/Valence）；(2) Gemini 2.5 Flash多模态LLM分析完整音频+文本；(3) TRUST-Pathos三代理LLM监督集成分数。使用Spearman秩相关评估一致性，并在EMO-DB上系统评估标准SER基准语料库的质量缺陷。

Result: Gemini Valence与TRUST-Pathos强相关（rho=+0.664, p<0.001），而emotion2vec Valence无显著相关（rho=+0.097, p=0.499）。标准SER基准语料库（EMO-DB）存在表演式语音、文化偏见和类别不兼容问题。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多模态分析在捕捉语义定义的政治情感方面显著优于纯声学模型，声学特征仅对低层唤醒度估计有参考价值。未来将扩展至包含面部表情和注视的视频分析。

Abstract: We investigate whether acoustic emotion recognition models can serve as proxies for the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, as operationalised by the TRUST multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipeline. Using a Bundestag plenary speech by Felix Banaszak (51 segments, 245 s) as a case study, we compare three analysis modalities: (1) emotion2vec_plus_large, an acoustic speech emotion recognition (SER) model whose continuous Arousal and Valence values are derived via post-hoc Russell Circumplex projection; (2) Gemini 2.5 Flash, an LLM analysing the full speech audio together with its transcript in an open-ended, context-aware fashion; and (3) TRUST-Pathos scores from a three-advocate LLM supervisor ensemble. Spearman rank correlations reveal that Gemini Valence correlates strongly with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), whereas emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). We further demonstrate, via a systematic quality evaluation of the Berlin Database of Emotional Speech (EMO-DB) using Gemini in an open-ended annotation paradigm, that standard SER benchmark corpora suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category incompatibility. Our results suggest that LLM-based multimodal analysis captures semantically defined political emotion substantially better than acoustic models alone, while acoustic features remain informative for low-level Arousal estimation. Future work will extend this approach to video-based analysis incorporating facial expression and gaze.

</details>


### [264] [HarnessAPI: A Skill-First Framework for Unified Streaming APIs and MCP Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22733)
*Edwin Jose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarnessAPI 是一个 Python 框架，通过将类型化的技能文件夹作为单一事实来源，自动从 handler.py 和 Pydantic 模式派生出流式 HTTP 端点（SSE）、OpenAPI/Swagger UI 和 MCP 工具，消除了为 LLM 工具重复维护 HTTP 端点和 MCP 注册的负担，相比手动维护的双栈实现减少了 74% 的样板代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每个作为 LLM 工具部署的 Python 函数需要同时维护两种形式：用于人机客户端和 CI 管道的 HTTP 端点，以及用于 Claude、Cursor 等代理运行时的 MCP 工具注册。虽然业务逻辑相同，但路由、验证、序列化、流处理和模式维护等配套机制完全不同，且随着代码演化会导致两者逐渐偏离。

Method: 提出 HarnessAPI 框架，将类型化的技能文件夹作为单一事实来源。从 handler.py 和 Pydantic 模式自动派生出流式 HTTP 端点（SSE）、OpenAPI/Swagger UI 和零配置 MCP 工具，在单进程中提供服务。采用双模式内容协商使同一 handler 无需修改即可同时服务 SSE 流和 JSON 返回客户端。通过动态代码生成机制确保 Pydantic 类型注解正确传播到 FastMCP 的检查层。

Result: 在六项代表性技能上使用 cloc 测量，HarnessAPI 相比手动维护的双栈实现（FastAPI 服务器 + FastMCP 服务器）减少了 74% 的框架层面样板代码。该框架继承自 FastAPI，拥有其完整的中间件、依赖注入和部署生态。

Conclusion: HarnessAPI 有效消除了 LLM 工具部署中的代码重复问题，显著降低维护成本，同时保持了 FastAPI 生态的兼容性。

Abstract: Every Python function deployed as an LLM tool must today exist in two forms: an HTTP endpoint for human-facing clients and CI pipelines, and an MCP tool registration for agent runtimes such as Claude and Cursor. These representations share business logic yet diverge in all the surrounding machinery (routing, validation, serialisation, streaming, and schema maintenance), and they drift apart as the underlying code evolves. We present HarnessAPI, a Python framework that eliminates this duplication by treating a typed skill folder as the single source of truth. From one handler.py plus Pydantic schemas, the framework automatically derives a streaming HTTP endpoint with Server-Sent Events, an interactive OpenAPI/Swagger UI, and a zero-configuration MCP tool, all served from a single process. Dual-mode content negotiation lets the same handler serve SSE-streaming and JSON-returning clients with no handler changes. A dynamic code-generation mechanism ensures Pydantic type annotations propagate correctly to FastMCP's inspection layer, resolving a technical limitation that prevents naive closure-based registration. Measured across six representative skills using cloc, HarnessAPI reduces framework-facing boilerplate by 74% compared with a manually maintained dual-stack implementation (FastAPI server + FastMCP server). HarnessAPI subclasses FastAPI, inheriting its full middleware, dependency-injection, and deployment ecosystem. It is available at https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/harnessapi and on PyPI (pip install harnessapi)

</details>


### [265] [Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22759)
*Girish Narayanswamy,Maxwell A. Xu,A. Ali Heydari,Samy Abdel-Ghaffar,Marius Guerard,Kara Vaillancourt,Zhihan Zhang,Jake Garrison,Levi Albuquerque,Dimitris Spathis,Hong Yu,Hamid Palangi,Xuhai "Orson" Xu,David G. T. Barrett,Joseph Breda,Jed McGiffin,Yubin Kim,Yuwei Zhang,Naghmeh Rezaei,Samuel Solomon,Karan Ahuja,Tim Althoff,Jake Sunshine,Ming-Zher Poh,Benjamin Yetton,Ari Winbush,Nicholas B. Allen,James M. Rehg,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Yun Liu,John Hernandez,Anupam Pathak,Conor Heneghan,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally,Pushmeet Kohli,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超过1万亿分钟、500万参与者无标签传感器数据预训练的可穿戴健康基础模型。通过联合扩展模型容量和预训练数据量，在35项健康预测任务上实现系统性性能提升，并展示了少样本学习、生成能力及基于LLM Agent的自主下游任务搜索，最终通过临床医生评分验证了个人健康助手的相关性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器数据虽丰富，但将其转化为个性化健康洞察面临三大挑战：高度表型多样性（个体健康基线、生理、生活方式差异大）、高质量标注数据稀缺（收集成本高、回顾性标注不可行），以及缺乏能将低层传感器信号表征为高层健康状态的通用表示。

Method: 1）在大规模（5百万参与者、>1万亿分钟）无标签传感器数据上预训练基础模型；2）联合扩展模型容量与预训练数据量；3）采用少样本学习范式进行标签高效的任务适配；4）部署LLM Agent教室自主搜索下游预测头架构；5）集成下游预测器至个人健康Agent（PHA），并通过1860份临床医生评分进行验证。

Result: 在涵盖心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康、生活方式和人口统计的35项健康预测任务上，模型性能随模型容量与数据量联合扩展而系统性提升。少样本学习实现标签高效的任务适配，LLM Agent自主搜索带来随LLM容量增加而提升的性能改进。PHA在1860份临床医生评分中展现出更相关、更具上下文感知且更安全的响应。

Conclusion: 人口规模的无标签传感器数据预训练能够学习到通用且鲁棒的健康表征，结合少样本学习、LLM驱动的架构搜索和智能Agent集成，可有效解决可穿戴健康领域的数据稀缺与个性化挑战，为构建安全、上下文感知的个人健康助手提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.

</details>


### [266] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了使用LLM生成形式化证明（Lean语言）解决开放数学问题的能力。最佳智能体以每个问题数百美元的成本自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了44/492个OEIS猜想，并已应用于组合学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究。基础智能体（交替LLM生成与Lean验证）也取得了Erdős问题的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数学推理中表现出色但不可靠，限制了其在数学研究中的应用。通过让LLM生成形式化证明（如Lean语言）可以缓解这一问题，但该方法解决开放问题的能力此前缺乏大规模评估。

Method: 设计了多种智能体方案：最强大的智能体自主生成Lean形式化证明；基础智能体交替进行LLM生成和Lean验证。在353个开放Erdős问题和492个OEIS猜想上进行大规模评估。

Result: 最佳智能体解决了9/353个Erdős问题（每个问题成本数百美元），证明了44/492个OEIS猜想；基础智能体复现了Erdős的成功但在最难问题上成本更高。

Conclusion: AI辅助的形式化证明搜索是解决开放数学问题的有效方法，不同智能体设计对其能力有显著影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [267] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LCGuard框架，通过在多智能体LLM系统中对共享KV缓存进行表示级变换，在保持任务性能的同时减少基于重构的敏感信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统中，KV缓存作为潜在通信通道会编码敏感信息（上下文输入、推理状态、智能体特定信息），导致敏感内容在不经文本披露的情况下在智能体间传播，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存工件跨智能体传输前学习表示级变换。采用对抗训练框架：对手学习重构敏感输入，LCGuard学习在保留任务相关语义的同时减少可重构信息量的变换。

Result: 跨多个模型家族和多智能体基准测试中，相比标准KV共享基线，LCGuard持续降低基于重构的泄露率和攻击成功率，同时保持有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard有效平衡了KV缓存通信的效率优势与隐私保护需求，为多智能体系统的安全潜在通信提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [268] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated DeltaNet-2，通过解耦擦除门和写入门（均为逐通道）来改进Delta规则线性注意力，解决了先前方法（Gated DeltaNet、KDA）中单个标量门控同时控制擦除和写入的限制，在1.3B参数、100B tokens训练下，在语言建模、常识推理、检索及长上下文RULER基准上取得优于Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA、Mamba-3的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（Gated DeltaNet、KDA）使用单个标量门控同时控制两个不同的操作：键侧的旧内容擦除和值侧的新内容写入。这种耦合限制了模型灵活编辑压缩记忆的能力，导致无法精细控制遗忘和写入。

Method: 提出Gated DeltaNet-2，引入逐通道的擦除门b_t和写入门w_t，解耦擦除与写入角色；推导了快速权重更新视角、带逐通道衰减的块状WY算法（非对称擦除因子），以及门控感知反向传播以实现高效并行训练。

Result: 在1.3B参数、100B FineWeb-Edu tokens训练下，Gated DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理、检索任务上整体最优，超越Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA、Mamba-3；在长上下文RULER基准的多键检索设置中优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 解耦擦除门和写入门能有效提升Delta规则线性注意力的记忆编辑能力，尤其在长上下文多键检索场景中表现突出，代码已开源。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


### [269] [MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22794)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Wei Xue,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSS是一个在源代码层面实现自主智能体系统自我演进的系统，通过自动收集生产环境故障证据、确定性多阶段流水线进行代码重写，并结合回放验证与容器热替换机制，在无需人工干预的情况下将OpenClaw四任务平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体系统部署后无法从用户交互中学习，重复性故障需等待人工更新修复；已有的自我进化方法仅限文本层（技能文件、提示配置等），而路由、钩子顺序、状态不变量等结构性问题存在于代码层，文本层无法触及这类故障。

Method: MOSS系统在源代码级别进行自我重写：首先自动批量收集生产环境故障证据，然后通过确定性多阶段流水线处理，将代码修改委托给可插拔的外部编码智能体CLI，MOSS保留阶段排序与裁决权。候选修改通过在临时试验worker中回放故障批次进行验证，通过后经用户同意、容器热替换上线，并配有健康检查门控的回滚机制。

Result: 在OpenClaw基准上，MOSS在单次演进周期内无需人工干预即将四任务平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。

Conclusion: 源代码层自适应比文本层修改更通用（图灵完备、是文本可修改范围的严格超集）、执行确定性更强（依赖确定性流水线而非基础模型遵从性）、且不会因长上下文漂移而退化，是自主智能体系统自我进化的更优介质。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [Adaptive RBF-KAN: A Comparative Evaluation of Dynamic Shape Parameters in Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21534)
*Roberto Cavoretto,Alessandra De Rossi,Adeeba Haider,Amir Noorizadegan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于RBF的KAN框架，引入了更广泛的径向基核函数族（包括Matérn和Wendland核），并通过留一法交叉验证（LOOCV）初始化核形状参数，在训练中进一步自适应优化。实验表明，不同核函数在处理光滑/不连续/振荡函数时各具优势，LOOCV初始化与自适应核学习相结合是提升RBF-KAN模型的有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FastKAN使用固定的高斯RBF核和形状参数，限制了模型的灵活性和表达能力。作者希望引入更丰富的核函数族并实现数据驱动的核参数初始化，以提升KAN模型的性能和适应性。

Method: 1) 将FastKAN中的高斯RBF扩展为更广泛的径向基核函数族（包括Matérn核和Wendland核）；2) 首次在深度KAN训练中集成LOOCV方法估计核形状参数作为初始化；3) 将LOOCV初始化的核尺度参数在后续网络训练中进一步微调优化。

Result: 在多个二维基准函数上的评估表明：不同核函数在不同类型的函数（平滑、不连续、振荡）上表现出各自的优势；自适应形状参数比固定参数效果更优；LOOCV初始化与自适应学习相结合能有效提升RBF-KAN性能。

Conclusion: LOOCV-based核参数初始化结合自适应核学习是为RBF-KAN模型提供实用改进的有效策略，核函数的选择和形状参数的自适应对模型性能有重要影响。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) approximate multivariate functions using learnable univariate edge functions, typically parameterized by B-spline bases. Although effective, spline-based implementations can be computationally expensive. A modified version of KANs, called FastKAN, improves efficiency by replacing splines with Gaussian radial basis functions (RBFs), but it relies on a fixed kernel and shape parameter. In this work, we extend the RBF-based KAN framework by introducing a broader family of radial basis kernels and by initializing the kernel shape parameter using leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that integrates LOOCV-based kernel scale estimation with deep KAN training. We also introduce Matérn and Wendland kernels into the KAN framework for the first time, enabling more flexible basis representations beyond the Gaussian kernel used in FastKAN. The LOOCV estimate provides a data-driven initialization of the kernel scale, which is subsequently refined during network training. The proposed adaptive RBF-KAN is evaluated on several two-dimensional benchmark functions. The results highlight the importance of kernel selection and adaptive shape parameters, with different kernels showing advantages for smooth functions, discontinuities, and oscillatory patterns. Overall, combining LOOCV-based initialization with adaptive kernel learning provides a practical strategy for improving RBF-based KAN models.

</details>


### [271] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需预处理假设和因果充分性假设的局部学习方法，用于选择协变量以无偏估计总因果效应，通过刻画局部边界并开发局部识别程序，在保证估计准确性的同时大幅提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协变量选择方法依赖全局因果结构学习或强假设（如因果充分性、预处理假设），这些假设在实践中常不成立，且全局学习在高维场景下计算成本过高。

Method: 首先刻画一个局部边界，该边界包含至少一个有效的调整集（若存在）；然后开发局部识别程序，在此边界内高效搜索有效的协变量集。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和两个真实数据集上，该方法实现了准确的因果效应估计，并显著提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的局部学习方法是可靠且完备的，能够在非参数因果效应估计中避免预处理和因果充分性假设，兼顾准确性与高效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [272] [Scalable On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Batch Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21557)
*Jongchan Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应批次缩放（ABS）方法，通过动态调整有效批次大小来应对强化学习中的非平稳性，打破了传统认为大批量训练不适用于RL的观点，在ALE基准测试上实现了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"大批量训练与RL不兼容"的传统认知，揭示非平稳性随训练阶段动态演变的特性，并利用这一特性设计自适应批次缩放方法。

Method: 提出Adaptive Batch Scaling (ABS)，核心是Behavioral Divergence指标——通过测量连续更新间的动作层面偏移来量化策略非平稳性，并根据策略波动性反向调节批次大小。

Result: 集成到PQN算法中在ALE基准上验证，发现更大的网络结合更大的批次尺寸能达到最佳性能，打破了RL中无法实现这种规模化行为的传统观点。

Conclusion: RL中的非平稳性是动态演变的，早期需要小批次保持塑性，后期可切换到大批次实现精准收敛；自适应批次控制能够解锁此前被认为在RL中不可实现的规模化行为。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom holds that large-batch training is fundamentally incompatible with Reinforcement Learning (RL) - beyond a modest threshold, increasing batch sizes typically yields diminishing returns or performance degradation due to the inherent non-stationarity of the data distribution. We challenge this view by observing that non-stationarity is not a fixed property of RL, but evolves throughout training: early stages exhibit rapid behavioral shifts that demand small batches for plasticity, whereas late stages approach a quasi-stationary regime where large batches enable precise convergence. Motivated by this observation, we propose Adaptive Batch Scaling (ABS), that dynamically adjusts the effective batch size according to the stability of the learning policy. Central to ABS is Behavioral Divergence, a novel metric that quantifies policy non-stationarity by measuring action-level shifts between consecutive updates, which we use to scale batch size inversely to policy volatility. Integrated with the Parallelised Q-Network (PQN) algorithm and evaluated on the ALE benchmark, ABS seamlessly reconciles early-stage plasticity with late-stage stable convergence. Strikingly, contrary to conventional wisdom, our results reveal that the combination of larger networks and larger batch sizes achieves the best performance - a scaling behavior previously thought to be unattainable in RL, now unlocked through adaptive batch control.

</details>


### [273] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持感知的离线决策框架，用于广告拍卖中的保留价策略选择。该框架不输出单一的点估计胜出策略，而是将日志证据转化为保守决策对象，包括认证策略、统计劣势策略和需要进一步验证的候选策略。实验表明，该框架能将19个策略缩减为2个验证候选，同时保证44个广告主、交易平台和地区细分市场中的无伤害认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的日志回放和离线策略评估方法仅能估计或排序策略价值，但无法直接回答"现有证据是否足够强以证明验证必要性"这一操作性问题。同时，回放表可能存在弱阈值支持、多重比较效应、子群体伤害和投标人响应不确定性等风险。

Method: 提出支持感知的离线决策框架，通过同时控制不确定性并施加保守支持门限，将日志证据转化为三类决策对象：认证策略、统计劣势策略和未解决候选策略。核心理论结果提供了统一的有限目录保证，确保框架保留最佳通过门限策略，仅剔除有认证遗憾的策略。此外还刻画了支持局部化回放泛化、建立信息论阈值分辨率极限、量化异构投标人响应对局部化回放排名的影响。

Result: 在iPinYou实时竞价日志上：领先保留价规则在第二季实现47.66%的回放提升、40.71%的同时下界提升，第三季实现43.87%的冻结超时回放提升。框架将19个策略目录缩减为2个策略的验证候选短列表，并在44个广告主、交易平台和地区细分市场中认证无伤害。

Conclusion: 离线保留价策略评估应产生认证验证决策，而非仅依赖点估计排名。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [274] [Uniform-in-Time Weak Propagation-of-Chaos in Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22010)
*Margalit Glasgow,Joan Bruna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了单隐层神经网络在梯度下降特征学习机制下的有限宽度网络与无限宽度平均场动力学之间的差异，利用平均场确定性Wasserstein梯度流动力学的收敛速率，在无需强凸性或对数Sobolev不等式等假设的条件下，建立了关于时间一致的非渐近弱传播混沌界，其泛化误差与神经元数量的关系为poly(d) m^{-min(1,c/6)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常利用Grönwall估计获得常数时间范围内的有限宽度与无限宽度网络间的误差界，但长时间行为分析较为困难。已有的时间一致界通常依赖于损失景观的(局部)强凸性或噪声梯度动力学中的对数Sobolev不等式，这些假设限制了适用场景。本文旨在不依赖这些几何假设，仅利用平均场确定性梯度流动力学的收敛速率来建立时间一致性的波动界。

Method: 利用平均场确定性Wasserstein梯度流动力学的收敛速率，在标准正则性假设及条件∫_0^∞ L_t^{1/2} dt = O(log d)下，建立时间一致的非渐近弱传播混沌界。核心推导基于收敛速率L_t ≲ t^{-c}，得到‖f_{ρ_t^{MF}} - f_{\hatρ_t^m}‖^2 ≲ poly(d) m^{-min(1,c/6)}。

Result: 建立了时间一致的非渐近弱传播混沌界：‖f_{ρ_t^{MF}} - f_{\hatρ_t^m}‖^2 ≲ poly(d) m^{-min(1,c/6)}，条件为L_t ≲ t^{-c}且∫_0^∞ L_t^{1/2} dt = O(log d)。当平均场总体损失动力学收敛速度快于t^{-2}时，仅需poly(d/ε)个神经元、训练样本和梯度下降步数即可达到ε的损失。

Conclusion: 本文在无噪声环境下，不依赖损失景观最优值附近的几何假设，建立了时间一致的传播混沌界，并推广至有限样本和时间离散化等其他离散化形式。结果表明当平均场收敛速率足够快时，网络能以多项式复杂度的资源达到任意精度ε的损失。

Abstract: We consider one-hidden layer neural networks trained in the feature-learning regime using gradient descent, and relate the output of the finite-width network $f_{\hatρ_t^m}$ to its infinite-width counterpart $f_{ρ_t^{MF}}$, which evolves in the mean-field dynamics.
  While constant-time horizon bounds for $\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}} - f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|$ may be obtained via standard Grönwall estimates, the long-time behavior of the fluctuation is a more delicate matter. Uniform-in-time bounds often rely on (local) strong convexity in the landscape or Logarithmic Sobolev inequalities present in noisy gradient dynamics.
  In this work, we establish non-asymptotic weak propagation-of-chaos that holds uniformly in time, obtained by exploiting instead the convergence rate of the mean-field deterministic Wasserstein-gradient-flow dynamics. Specifically, denoting by $L_t$ the mean-field excess MSE loss at time $t$ and $m$ the number of neurons, under standard regularity assumptions and the condition $\int_0^\infty L_t^{1/2} dt =O(\log d)$, we obtain the uniform in time bound $\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}}- f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|^2 \lesssim \text{poly}(d) m^{-\min(1,c/6)}$ whenever $L_t \lesssim t^{-c}$. Our result holds in a noiseless setting and does not make any assumptions on the geometry of the landscape near the optimum, and extends seamlessly to other forms of discretization, including finite number of samples and time discretization. A key takeaway of our result is that whenever the convergence rate of the mean-field, population-loss dynamics is faster than $t^{-2}$, we can attain a loss of $ε$ with only $\text{poly}(d/ε)$ neurons, training samples, and GD steps.

</details>


### [275] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是2017-2018年的一份技术报告，利用在线投注策略的财富保证推导了迭代对数定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用在线投注策略的财富保证来证明迭代对数定律，提供一种新的概率论证明方法。

Method: 通过分析在线投注策略的财富增长特性，建立与迭代对数定律之间的数学联系。

Result: 成功利用投注策略的财富保证推导出迭代对数定律。

Conclusion: 在线投注策略的博弈论方法可以作为一种有效的工具来证明经典的迭代对数定律。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [276] [Departure from Regularity: Degree Heterogeneity and Eigengap as the Structural Drivers of ASE-LSE Latent Subspace Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22346)
*Minh Triet Pham,Ian Gallagher*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了邻接谱嵌入与拉普拉斯谱嵌入产生不同结果的结构性原因：正则性（每个节点度数相同）确保两者完全一致；度数异质性驱动差异，社区结构强度抑制差异，二者比值可预测嵌入可互换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻接谱嵌入和拉普拉斯谱嵌入是图数据分析中两种最广泛使用的方法，但它们在相同网络上常产生不同结果，其背后的结构原因尚未被完全理解，本文旨在提供结构性解释。

Method: 首先证明正则性是两种方法产生完全一致潜在子空间的充分条件；然后推导出偏离正则性时两者差异的显式界限，该界限包含两项：度数异质性和社区结构强度；最后通过数千个模拟网络进行实证验证。

Result: 度数异质性驱动两种嵌入方法的差异增大，社区结构强度抑制差异；两者之比是预测嵌入可互换性的强预测因子。

Conclusion: 当网络具有正则性时，两种嵌入等价；否则需根据度数异质性与社区结构强度的比值判断能否互换使用。

Abstract: Two of the most widely used methods for analysing graph data, Adjacency Spectral Embedding and Laplacian Spectral Embedding, often produce different results when applied to the same network. Yet the structural reasons behind this disagreement remain incompletely understood. This paper provides a structural account. We show that regularity is a sufficient condition for perfect agreement: when every node has the same number of connections, the two methods produce identical latent subspaces. Any departure from this regularity introduces disagreement, and we prove an explicit bound whose two terms suggest the structural ingredients controlling it: degree heterogeneity, which pushes the methods apart, and community structure strength, which pulls them back together. We validate both drivers empirically across thousands of simulated networks, confirming that heterogeneity drives disagreement up, community strength suppresses it, and their ratio provides a strong predictor of when the two embeddings can be treated as interchangeable and when they cannot.

</details>


### [277] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复第一价格拍卖中的“刷量”（Shilling）行为——即通过虚假出价使竞争看似更激烈从而推高价格。这种操纵只影响学习者的观测（输时看到的是真实出价与刷量出价的最大值），不影响分配结果。在已知刷量分布的前提下，我们提出了结合鲁棒区间消除分支（动态定价率$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$）和乐观分支（去偏后利用后缀信息，达到第一价格拍卖率$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$）的算法，并通过验证与竞赛机制自适应选择。上下界匹配，证明即使仅影响反馈的刷量也能显著改变重复竞价的统计难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在重复第一价格拍卖中，刷量（Shilling）这种仅影响学习者观测反馈（不影响分配结果）的操纵行为如何改变学习者的出价学习过程，并分析其带来的统计难度变化。

Method: 提出双分支算法：1）鲁棒区间消除分支，忽略刷量报告，达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$遗憾；2）乐观分支，对输方报告进行去偏，在可靠时利用后缀信息，达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾。通过验证与竞赛（validation and racing）机制，在不预知尺度和反馈几何的条件下自适应选择分支。

Result: 算法达到了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$的自适应遗憾上界，并在单活跃区域情况下给出了匹配的对数因子下界。

Conclusion: 仅影响反馈的刷量行为就能显著改变重复竞价的统计难度，即使刷量分布已知，学习者仍需面对更复杂的权衡。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>


### [278] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对HSIC及其联合独立扩展dHSIC的排列检验计算成本高的问题，提出了两种学生化统计量mHSIC和mdHSIC，其零分布为标准正态，无需排列校准，计算速度提升25-60倍，且保持相同的检验功效和第一类错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSIC和dHSIC作为退化的V统计量，其零分布为数据依赖的加权卡方分布，实践中需通过排列检验校准，导致每次检验成本乘以排列次数（通常两个数量级），计算开销巨大。

Method: (1) mHSIC：自归一化的下三角和，使用两个经验中心化Gram矩阵的Hadamard积，零分布渐近标准正态，复杂度为二次方；(2) mdHSIC：半样本分裂，一半估计中心化，另一半运行下三角自归一化鞅，将条件均值残差压缩为d指数小的量，复杂度随d线性增长。

Result: 在合成数据上（每变量输入维度1-500，联合测试变量2-10），两种统计量均匹配排列校准基线的经验第一类错误率和检验功效，运行速度提升25-60倍。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过学生化构造使得零分布为标准正态，彻底消除排列校准需求，大幅降低计算成本，且保持统计一致性，适用于高维联合独立性检验场景。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


### [279] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于一步生成建模的保守漂移方法，利用KDE梯度速度替代原始位移速度以解决非保守性问题，并给出了连续时间有限粒子收敛界，分析了非保守拉普拉斯核方法，最后将残余速度界转化为一步生成保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一般基于位移的漂移场存在的非保守性问题，提出一种保守漂移方法，以实现更稳定的一步生成建模。

Method: 将原始位移速度替换为KDE梯度速度（核平滑数据得分与核平滑模型得分之差），并针对非保守方法引入拉普拉斯核与伴生核分解。

Result: 证明了保守方法在R^d上的连续时间有限粒子收敛界，包括经验Stein漂移、平滑Fisher散度和平方中心速度的界；给出了残余速度率的显式表达式（N^{-1/(d+4)} 和 N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}）；分析了非保守Laplace核方法的不可避免残差项。

Conclusion: 保守漂移方法有效解决了非保守性问题，连续时间残余速度界可通过显式漂移大小η转化为一步生成质量保证。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>
